# PLAN.md - team nuzukm, Blender MCP

File of record. Owner: Grace (Planner/API). Contract changes are versioned amendments
in section 8 and are re-posted in #team-nuzukm. Builders build to the Lead's stated
reading until a re-post lands.

Plan version: A.4 / B0.3 / B1.1 / B2.1 (2026-09-11); D1.6 (team nosubl, 2026-10-16, section 12). Branch: `blender-5.2` (cut from `main` at `ef0ff7d`).
Phase A = migration to Blender 5.2.1 LTS, version 2.0.0. Phase B = feature requests,
planned after Phase A slices are out (section 6).

## 0. House rules (Lead's kickoff post, verbatim)

1. Inbox first on every wake. ACK every assignment / finding / ruling / gate by DM within one poll. No ACK in 5 min = re-assigned.
2. One file = one owner. addon.py = Ton. server.py + __init__.py = Campbell. tests/ = Bastien. TOOLS.md, README.md, docs/*.md (not PLAN.md), pyproject.toml, /blender skill, memory note = Sybren. docs/PLAN.md = Grace. Cross-file needs go through me.
3. Contract changes only as versioned amendments in PLAN.md, re-posted here. Build to my stated reading until the re-post lands.
4. 4.x/5.x differences are resolved by try-assign or hasattr, NEVER by comparing bpy.app.version. Grep found TWO existing version comparisons in addon.py (lines 2439 and 4414): both go.
5. Evidence = pasted headless output, PASS/FAIL per test-plan step, never a summary. Assertions are never weakened. Image results pass a non-blank pixel test (stddev > 0), not eyeballing.
6. Report "done" only when the FULL harness ran on BOTH installs and the output is pasted. I re-run it myself before accepting.

Lead rulings that PLAN.md restates and does not reopen: branch `blender-5.2`, nobody
commits, deploys, launches a Blender GUI or the MCP server; live copies
(`B:\...`, `D:\blender-4.3.2-windows-x64\MCP\`, `%APPDATA%` add-ons) are never edited;
headless command is `<install>\blender.exe --background --factory-startup --python <script>`
with `<install>` = `D:\blender-4.3.2-windows-x64` and `D:\blender-5.2.1-windows-x64`;
`bl_info["blender"]` stays `(4, 0, 0)`; `material.use_nodes` / `world.use_nodes` stay.

Shared-worktree rule (Lead, 11:21): NO `git stash` / `checkout` / `reset` by anyone,
Lead included, while a builder is mid-slice; compare against `main` with
`git show main:<file>` into the scratchpad.

8. (Lead, 11:55) NO write to a shared-tree file without a room post carrying the new `git hash-object` in the SAME minute; a landing that is not posted does not exist for verification. The Verifier pins whichever hash is on disk when a run STARTS and quotes it.
7. (Lead, 11:27) Nobody re-checks-out, stashes or resets a file under another owner; review diffs with `git diff --ignore-cr-at-eol`; any file hash used as evidence is `git hash-object <file>` (normalised), never a raw sha256 of the working copy (autocrlf rewrote server.py/__init__.py to CRLF at 11:20, content intact). Builders quote both when they quote a sha; the harness header uses git hash-object.

## 2. Phase A contract (per hard break)

Evidence lines: `docs/apichecks/out_4.3.2.jsonl` and `out_5.2.1.jsonl`, tag `MIGCHECK`
(script `docs/apichecks/migcheck.py`, run 2026-09-11 on both installs; it loads the
repo's addon.py, registers it headless and calls the four affected handlers). Every
fact below is "fact / evidence key / versions".

### 2.0 A1 rulings R1-R6 (Lead to Ton, m_a86d329a86a6062e2d7b, verbatim)

R1 boolean_operation: the live solver enum is only knowable inside Blender, so the mapping lives in the ADDON; the server passes the uppercased string through. Accept EXACT/FAST/FLOAT case-insensitively. If the enum lacks the requested id, map FAST<->FLOAT; reply includes "solver": <id actually set>; unknown value -> {"error": "solver 'X' not valid; valid: [live enum items]"}. Read the enum from mod.bl_rna.properties['solver'].enum_items.
R2 set_render_settings: try-assign the user's engine id, then the alias (BLENDER_EEVEE <-> BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT), never bpy.app.version. Reply includes "engine": scene.render.engine (the resolved id). On failure list scene.render.bl_rna.properties['engine'].enum_items ids in the error and note that add-on engines (Cycles) may be missing from that list. Lowercase input accepted.
R3 render_depth_map: `if hasattr(tmp, "compositing_node_group")` -> node group path (bpy.data.node_groups.new, NodeGroupOutput + interface Image socket) else scene.node_tree path. Node ids: try Compositor id then Shader id via a small helper _new_node(tree, *ids) that lists what it tried in the error. Remove the node group AND the temp scene in finally; the Verifier asserts node_groups and scenes counts unchanged after the call. Line 761 loop: reorder to BLENDER_EEVEE first, keep enum resolution.
R4 media_type: set image_settings.media_type = 'IMAGE' before file_format at 805, 1571, 2449, each guarded by hasattr; _render_settings saves/restores media_type in the same tuple (guarded).
R5 line 4414: _op_exists(bpy.ops.wm.obj_import) else import_scene.obj. Final grep for bpy.app.version outside bl_info must be 0.
R6 use_nodes lines: untouched.

Also from the same DM: bl_info["version"] = (2, 0, 0), bl_info["blender"] stays (4, 0, 0).

Later deltas accepted by the Lead: R2 error list built from the parsed live tuple (D22);
R3 Workbench fallback is `_set_render_engine(tmp.render, 'BLENDER_EEVEE')` try-assign;
R4 media type is `VIDEO` for FFMPEG and `MULTI_LAYER_IMAGE` for OPEN_EXR_MULTILAYER, not
always `IMAGE` (2.4 matrix); EXR/JPG/TIF aliases (D13); F6 guards (D18); MANIFOLD (D19).

### 2.1 Break #1: render engine identifier (addon.py `set_render_settings` ~2437-2441, `render_depth_map` ~757-765)

Facts:
- `BLENDER_EEVEE` assigns on 5.2.1 and raises `TypeError` on 4.3.2; `BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT` the reverse. `BLENDER_WORKBENCH` and `CYCLES` assign on both. / `engine_assign` / 4.3.2 + 5.2.1.
- The exception text is `bpy_struct: item.attr = val: enum "X" not found in ('BLENDER_EEVEE', 'BLENDER_WORKBENCH', 'CYCLES')` (5.2.1) and `... ('BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT', 'BLENDER_WORKBENCH', 'CYCLES')` (4.3.2): the tuple in the message IS the live enum. / `engine_assign` / both.
- Live `set_render_settings(engine="BLENDER_EEVEE")` on 5.2.1: `Exception: enum "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT" not found`. On 4.3.2 it succeeds and reports `BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT`. / `addon_register.handler_smoke.set_render_settings_eevee` / both.

Idiom (addon helpers, as implemented by Ton; D11):

```python
_ENGINE_ALIASES = {'BLENDER_EEVEE': ('BLENDER_EEVEE', 'BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT'),
                   'BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT': ('BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT', 'BLENDER_EEVEE')}

@classmethod
def _set_render_engine(cls, render, engine):
    """Try-assign, case-insensitive, accepts bare EEVEE / CYCLES / WORKBENCH (BLENDER_ prefix
    added) and the EEVEE alias pair. Returns an error dict on failure, else None.
    Never inspects bpy.app.version."""
```

Contract:
- `set_render_settings(engine=...)`: `if engine is not None:` call the helper and return
  its error dict as the reply on failure (covers M3 `engine="BOGUS"`, M13 `engine=""`,
  lower-case `eevee`). Reply keeps `engine` = `scene.render.engine`, the resolved live id.
  The `samples` branch keeps its string membership test on both EEVEE ids.
- Error text (D22, accepted): `_engine_ids()` probes by assignment and parses the live
  tuple out of the `TypeError` (static `enum_items` as fallback), so the `valid:` list
  names WORKBENCH and CYCLES on both versions despite the D9 blind spot.
- `render_depth_map`: Workbench fallback is `_set_render_engine(tmp.render, 'BLENDER_EEVEE')`
  (try-assign; the alias table reaches BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT on 4.x).
- `engine=None` = unchanged, `engine=""` = error (D17).

### 2.2 Break #2: compositor tree in `render_depth_map` (addon.py ~774-800)

Facts:
- 5.2.1: `Scene.node_tree` absent, `Scene.compositing_node_group` present and `None` after `scene.copy()` on a factory scene; the source scene's group stays `None` after the temp scene got its own tree. 4.3.2: `node_tree` present, no `compositing_node_group`. / `depth_map.src_has_*`, `tmp_comp_group_after_copy`, `src_comp_group_untouched` / both.
- Live handler on 5.2.1: `Exception: 'Scene' object has no attribute 'node_tree'`. / `handler_smoke.render_depth_map` / 5.2.1.
- `CompositorNodeMapRange` undefined on 5.2.1; `ShaderNodeMapRange` accepted in a `CompositorNodeTree`. On 4.3.2 `CompositorNodeMapRange` exists and `ShaderNode*` ids are rejected in the compositor (nodecheck A errors). / `depth_map.map_range_id`, nodecheck / both.
- `ShaderNodeMapRange` (5.2.1) inputs `["Value", "From Min", "From Max", "To Min", "To Max", "Steps", "Vector", "From Min", ...]` (float set first, then vector set; `inputs["From Min"]` resolves to the float one), outputs `["Result", "Vector"]`, clamp property is `clamp`. `CompositorNodeMapRange` (4.3.2) inputs `["Value", "From Min", "From Max", "To Min", "To Max"]`, outputs `["Value"]`, property `use_clamp`. / `depth_map.map_range_*` / both.
- `CompositorNodeInvert` exists on both. Inputs 4.3.2 `["Fac", "Color"]`, 5.2.1 `["Color", "Factor", "Invert Color", "Invert Alpha"]`; output `["Color"]` on both. Link by name `"Color"`. / `depth_map.invert_*` / both.
- `CompositorNodeComposite` gone on 5.2.1; `NodeGroupOutput` after `tree.interface.new_socket(name="Image", in_out="OUTPUT", socket_type="NodeSocketColor")` exposes input `"Image"`. / `depth_map.output_*` / 5.2.1.
- `CompositorNodeRLayers.outputs` on 5.2.1 lists only enabled passes: `["Image", "Alpha", "Depth"]` after `use_pass_z = True` (set it BEFORE creating the node, as the code already does). 4.3.2 lists all 31. / `depth_map.rl_outputs` / both.
- The rebuilt graph renders on both: `render: ["FINISHED"]`, file written, pixels vary on 5.2.1 (max 0.208); after cleanup `bpy.data.node_groups` is empty. / `depth_map.render`, `pixel_varies`, `node_groups_left` / both.

Idiom (inside `render_depth_map`, as implemented; D11):

```python
group = None
try:
    ...                                   # use_pass_z on every view layer FIRST
    if hasattr(tmp, "compositing_node_group"):
        group = bpy.data.node_groups.new(f"{tmp.name}_tree", "CompositorNodeTree")
        group.interface.new_socket(name="Image", in_out='OUTPUT', socket_type='NodeSocketColor')
        tmp.compositing_node_group = group
        tree = group
    else:
        tmp.use_nodes = True
        tree = tmp.node_tree
    rl = self._new_node(tree, "CompositorNodeRLayers")
    map_node = self._new_node(tree, "CompositorNodeMapRange", "ShaderNodeMapRange")
    map_node.inputs["From Min"/"From Max"/"To Min"/"To Max"].default_value = ...   # first match = float socket
    for clamp_attr in ("use_clamp", "clamp"): setattr if hasattr
    map_out = map_node.outputs.get("Value") or map_node.outputs.get("Result")
    invert = self._new_node(tree, "CompositorNodeInvert", "ShaderNodeInvert")
    out_node = self._new_node(tree, "NodeGroupOutput" if group is not None else "CompositorNodeComposite")
    depth_out = rl.outputs.get("Depth") or rl.outputs.get("Z")
    links: depth_out -> map_node.inputs["Value"]; map_out -> invert.inputs["Color"]; invert.outputs["Color"] -> out_node.inputs["Image"]
    err = self._set_file_format(tmp.render.image_settings, 'PNG'); if err: return err
finally:
    remove tmp scene; if group is not None: bpy.data.node_groups.remove(group, do_unlink=True)
```

with the helper `_new_node(tree, *ids)`: `tree.nodes.new()` with the first id this Blender
accepts (compositor id first, shader twin second); raises `RuntimeError` naming every id
tried when none exists.

Contract: reply shape unchanged (`success, filepath, max_depth`). Cleanup guarantees for
M4: scene count and node-group count equal their before-values after the call, on both
versions, including the error paths (no camera, render failure, missing node id).

### 2.3 Break #3: boolean solver (addon.py ~2397-2415; server.py `boolean_operation` ~3297)

Facts:
- Solver enum is static and complete (not a dynamic blind spot): 4.3.2 `["FAST", "EXACT"]`, 5.2.1 `["FLOAT", "EXACT", "MANIFOLD"]`. Assigning a missing id raises `TypeError` with the live tuple. / `boolean_solver` / both.
- Live `boolean_operation(solver="FAST")` on 5.2.1: `Exception: enum "FAST" not found in ('FLOAT', 'EXACT', 'MANIFOLD')`; the modifier is left on the target. / `handler_smoke.boolean_fast` / 5.2.1.

Idiom (addon, as implemented; D11): `solver_id = solver.strip().upper()`; `solvers_valid`
from `mod.bl_rna.properties['solver'].enum_items` (static and complete here, unlike D9);
try `solver_id` then its alias (`FAST <-> FLOAT`); first hit is assigned and recorded as
`resolved_solver`; no hit removes the modifier and returns
`{"error": "solver '<x>' not valid; valid: [...] (FAST and FLOAT are accepted as aliases of each other)"}`.
`operation` is validated the same way against its enum; a non-MESH target is an error;
apply restores the previous selection and active object in `finally`.

Contract:
- Reply ADDS `"solver": <live id>` (read before apply). `operation` is echoed upper-cased,
  `applied` is a bool. Existing keys unchanged.
- Server (`boolean_operation` wrapper, already on the branch): docstring
  `solver: EXACT, FLOAT (FAST accepted as alias), MANIFOLD (Blender 5.2+)`; passes the
  string through unchanged; confirmation string appends the reply's `solver`. Error path
  unchanged (`Error: <text>`), which carries the enum.

### 2.4 Break #4: `ImageFormatSettings.media_type` (addon.py 805, 1571, 2449; `_render_settings` 1558-1583)

Facts:
- `media_type` exists on 5.2.1 (enum `["IMAGE", "MULTI_LAYER_IMAGE", "VIDEO"]`, default `IMAGE`), absent on 4.3.2. / `media_type.has_media_type`, `media_type_enum` / both.
- 5.2.1 assignment matrix: under `IMAGE`: `PNG`, `OPEN_EXR` assign; `OPEN_EXR_MULTILAYER` and `FFMPEG` raise. Under `MULTI_LAYER_IMAGE`: only `OPEN_EXR_MULTILAYER` assigns (`not found in ('OPEN_EXR_MULTILAYER')`). Under `VIDEO`: only `FFMPEG` assigns (`not found in ('FFMPEG')`). `file_format` `enum_items` reads the full 16-entry list under every media type (D10). 4.3.2: every format assigns directly. / `media_matrix`, `media_type.*` / both.
- `Image.file_format` (datablock, used by the resize helper at 530 and 709) assigns `PNG` on both; not affected. / `media_type.image_datablock_file_format` / both.
- Still renders (Workbench and EEVEE) write PNG headless on both versions; the live `render_from_camera` succeeds on 5.2.1 without any change. / `render_still`, `handler_smoke.render_from_camera` / both.

Idiom (addon helper `_set_file_format(image_settings, file_format)`, as implemented;
D11; replaces the three direct assignments): `fmt = file_format.strip().upper()`; when
`hasattr(image_settings, "media_type")`, try-assign the media type from
`{'FFMPEG': ('VIDEO',), 'OPEN_EXR_MULTILAYER': ('MULTI_LAYER_IMAGE', 'MULTI_LAYER')}.get(fmt, ('IMAGE',))`
(the matrix above proves all three mappings); aliases `EXR -> OPEN_EXR`, `JPG -> JPEG`,
`TIF -> TIFF` are applied first (D13); then assign `file_format`; on failure return
`{"error": "file_format '<x>' not valid; valid: [...]"}` with the static, complete
16-entry enum (D22; the assignment `TypeError` would name only the formats the current
media type accepts, D10).

`_render_settings` saves `getattr(ims, "media_type", None)` alongside `file_format`
and restores in the order media_type THEN file_format (restoring `PNG` while
`media_type` is still `VIDEO` raises); a helper error inside the context manager is
raised as `ValueError` so the caller's `except` reports it. `set_render_settings(file_format=...)`
returns the helper's error dict.

### 2.5 Extra version compare (addon.py 4414, Hunyuan OBJ import)

`if bpy.app.version >= (4, 0, 0): wm.obj_import else import_scene.obj` becomes
`if self._op_exists(bpy.ops.wm.obj_import): ... else: ...`. `_op_exists` already exists
(line 618). After this, M7 (`grep "bpy.app.version" addon.py` = 0 hits) holds.

### 2.6 Non-breaks confirmed (no code change, tests must not assert the 4.3 value)

- `material.use_nodes = True` / `world.use_nodes = True` run without error on 5.2.1; on 5.2.1 the node tree already exists at creation (`mat_node_tree_on_create: true`, `use_nodes` default `True`), on 4.3.2 it does not. Keep the assignments (house rule). / `material_nodes` / both.
- addon.py imports, `register()` and `unregister()` run headless on 5.2.1 with `--factory-startup`; the panel class registers, `scene.blendermcp_port` / `blendermcp_server_running` exist. / `addon_register.panel_registered`, `scene_props` / both.
- `requests` 2.32.3 and numpy 2.3.4 bundled on 5.2.1 (2.27.1 / 1.24.3 on 4.3.2). / `misc` / both.
- `numpy.array(mathutils.Vector)` is `float32` on 5.2.1, `float64` on 4.3.2; `dtype=numpy.float64` gives float64 on both. Today neither addon.py nor server.py converts a mathutils value through numpy (server's numpy use is PIL-image based in `diff_images`); M12 is a grep with an expected 0 hits, kept as a guard for Phase B. / `misc.numpy_vector_dtype` / both.
- 5.2.1 only: `gpu.init`, `Window.screenshot`, `bpy.app.handlers.exit_pre`, `render.render(frame_start=, frame_end=)` exist. Not used in Phase A. `gpu.init()` does NOT make viewport captures work headless (D21); `Window.screenshot()` is live-session only. / `misc`; Sybren F5 / 5.2.1.
- `bpy.data.is_dirty` is `False` at 5.2.1 startup and `True` on 4.3.2 (scheck `data_flags`). Tests must not assume either.

### 2.8 Break #5 (A1.1): `ShaderNodeSeparateRGB` in `set_texture` (addon.py ~3302), and F8

Facts (SEPCHK `--python-expr` on both installs, material node tree, 2026-09-11; not saved to the
out files because the M10 baseline is frozen):
- 4.3.2: `ShaderNodeSeparateRGB` inputs `["Image"]`, outputs `["R", "G", "B"]`; `ShaderNodeSeparateColor` inputs `["Color"]`, outputs `["Red", "Green", "Blue"]`, `mode = 'RGB'`; `CombineRGB` / `CombineColor` mirror them.
- 5.2.1: `ShaderNodeSeparateRGB` and `ShaderNodeCombineRGB` are `Node type undefined`; `ShaderNodeSeparateColor` / `CombineColor` present with the same Red/Green/Blue sockets and `mode`. `ShaderNodeMixRGB` still present on 5.2.1 (deprecated; first input is `Factor` there, `Fac` on 4.3.2).
- `tempfile._cleanup` is absent on both bundled Pythons (F8).

Contract (ruled, Ton's F7 form, landed in D29): `self._new_node(tree, "ShaderNodeSeparateRGB", "ShaderNodeSeparateColor")`
and read each channel by name with a per-version fallback (`outputs.get("R") or outputs.get("Red")`,
same for G/Green, B/Blue), including the AO mix link. F8: `tempfile._cleanup()` replaced by an
explicit unlink of the HDRI temp file in `finally` (guarded).

### 2.9 A1.1 pre-existing defects F1, F2 (from G3)

- F1 `extrude_faces`: after extruding a single face the original cap face remains inside
  the mesh (12 verts / 11 faces; a correct single-face extrude of a 6-face cube gives 10
  faces). Fix in the BMesh path: delete the source faces (`bmesh.ops.delete(bm, geom=faces, context='FACES')`
  after `extrude_face_region`, or use `extrude_discrete_faces` and remove the originals), on
  both versions. Test: face count 10, vert count 12, manifold.
- F2 active/selection restore: `subdivide_mesh`, `apply_modifier`, `export_object`, `set_origin`,
  `set_smooth_shading` must restore `view_layer.objects.active` (and selection) in `finally`,
  the pattern Ton already used in `boolean_operation(apply=True)`. `add_primitive`,
  `import_file`, `join_objects` keep the result active; `separate_mesh` keeps the source
  active (D26).


Gate rule: a gate is a DM to the Lead with pasted headless output; the Lead posts the
gate result in the room. Grace answers design or API questions at any time with a
section number and, for API questions, a migcheck/apicheck evidence key.

## 9. Phase B0 slice amendment (B0.1): settings, save and load

Starts after the Lead posts "A1.1 accepted" (D28; assignments pre-issued to all four builders with that start condition). Contract = `docs/FEATURE-REQUEST-settings-save-load.md`
sections 0.1 (five required fixes), 1 (fixes table), 2.1 (Tier 1 tables: file lifecycle,
settings generic, typed conveniences, presets and profiles, add-ons/workspaces/preferences,
session state), 3 (shared helper), 4 (ripple points), 6 (tests 1-18), plus
`docs/FEATURE-REQUEST-update-notification.md` section 2.1 items 2-3 (`get_version`
command + tool, `PROTOCOL`). Tier 2 and 3 are OUT. Architecture rules unchanged
(rigging doc sections 0, 1, 4, 7). Version 2.1.0 in the three sources.

### 9.1 Contract deltas (versioned; the doc text stands where not listed)

| # | Delta | Ruled by |
|---|---|---|
| C1 | Target version 2.1.0 (doc says 1.7.x). | Lead |
| C2 | SEAM AUTHORITY (Lead 11:52): the LANDED `get_version` reply keys are exactly `{addon, blender, python, protocol, addon_file, background}`; Ton's seam DM naming `addon_version` / `blender_version` is superseded, Campbell builds the server tool on these keys. `get_version`: addon command `get_version -> {"addon": "2.1.0", "blender": bpy.app.version_string, "python": ..., "protocol": PROTOCOL}`; addon module constant `PROTOCOL = 1` (first numbered protocol; bumped by any later wire change); server constant `PROTOCOL = 1`; server tool `get_version` returns server version (from `__version__`), addon version or `"unreachable"`, Blender version, both protocol numbers with a `protocol_match` bool, the loaded `server.py` path, the settings file path, and `last_update_check: null` (the check itself is B8+). | Lead D23; Grace numbers |
| C3 | Doc test 1 asserts `is_dirty True` at factory startup; 5.2.1 measures False (4.3.2 True). Assert `is_saved False` and `filepath ""`, record `is_dirty`. `get_file_state` reports it, never assumes it. | Lead |
| C4 | `scene.eevee.use_gtao` and `view_settings.use_hdr_view` are removed on 5.2 (scheck diff). `describe/get/set_settings` are `bl_rna`-driven so they list only what exists; shipped presets (`game_bake`, `preview`, `final_eevee`, `final_cycles`, `sprite_sheet`, `turntable_video`) must not contain either key; `load_preset` reports unknown keys as `unset` and still applies the rest. | Lead |
| C5 | File compression default is ON in 5.0. `save_blend(compress=None)` = preference `use_file_compression` (as the doc says) and the reply reports the effective `compress`; doc test 2 passes `compress=True` and `compress=False` explicitly on both files. | Lead |
| C6 | `active_asset_library` index shift (5.2 adds "All Libraries" and "Essentials" at 0 and 1): Tier 2 (`set_asset_libraries`) only; no B0 code reads that index. | Lead |
| C7 | Settings precedence for the server: environment (`BLENDER_HOST`, `BLENDER_PORT`, `BLENDER_EXE`, `IMG_TO_3D_*`) ABOVE `~/.blender_mcp/settings.json`, file above code defaults. `_find_blender_exe` order becomes hint > env > settings file > PATH > pool (A2 order amended at B0 start). | Lead |
| C8 | Deploy table (docs only): 5.2 add-ons path `%APPDATA%\Blender Foundation\Blender\5.2\scripts\addons\`; there is no `D:\blender-5.2.1-windows-x64\MCP\` yet. Which live folders receive 2.x is a user decision recorded by Sybren, not built in B0. | Lead |
| C9 | The server-side cross-cutting change lands ONCE in one edit: a settings module that backs `DEFAULT_HOST`, `DEFAULT_PORT`, `_SAFE_IMAGE_MAX_PIXELS`, `_IMG_TO_3D_PORT`, the two 180 s socket timeouts, and a new `connect_timeout` (absent today) from the settings file with env override. | Lead |
| C10 | `run_on_files` is Tier 2 (out of B0); when built it stays synchronous per file. Sidecars `<file>.versions.json` and `<file>.mcp-profile.json` are written by the ADDON (it owns the file path); the server writes only under `~/.blender_mcp/` (settings, presets, session states). | Lead |
| C11 | Shared tools `set_frame_range` (rigging doc), `set_scene_units` (engine-readiness doc), `append_from_blend` (presentation doc) are built in B0, one owner each side (Ton handler, Campbell wrapper); the later docs consume them. | Lead |
| C12 | M9 rule for B0: no pre-existing tool name or parameter removed or retyped; adding params and tools is expected (`save_blend` +7, `load_blend` +4, `set_render_settings` +13, `start_blender` +2 per Campbell's recon, second-hand). Baseline re-cut as `tests/baseline_tools_2.1.txt` after B0-G3. The freeze on `get_blender_status` / `start_blender` lifts at the B0 assignment. | Lead |
| C13 | `enable_addon` / `disable_addon`: the doc's `addon_utils.enable(module, default_set=persist)` is wrong. On BOTH versions `default_set=False` registers the module but never adds it to `preferences.addons` (so `list_addons(enabled_only)` cannot see it and Rigify's own `register()` fails with `KeyError: key "rigify" not found`); `default_set=True` adds it and marks `preferences.is_dirty`. Contract: always `default_set=True` (and `disable(..., default_set=True)`); `persist` only decides whether `wm.save_userpref` is called afterwards (consent rule unchanged); reply reports `preferences.is_dirty`. A failed enable leaves classes half-registered (a retry raises `already registered as a subclass`): on failure call `addon_utils.disable(module, default_set=True)` inside `suppress` before returning the error. | Grace, evidence b0check `addons.enable_disabled_modules`, ENCHK runs |
| C14 | `save_blend(incremental=True)`: Blender's own naming is `a.blend -> a1.blend` on both versions (not `a_001`); a plain save afterwards leaves `a1.blend1` (the `save_version` preference default 1). Doc test 5 asserts `a1.blend`. | Grace, evidence b0check `save_roundtrip.save_incremental` |
| C15 | `revert_file` on 4.3.2 headless: a bare save/open/revert, save/incremental/revert and save/copy/save/revert all FINISH, but the full b0check sequence (save-as compressed, save-as copy uncompressed, incremental, plain save, revert) crashed Blender 4.3.2 with `EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION` while re-reading `a1.blend`; 5.2.1 finishes. Not pinned further. The harness runs its revert step in a fresh process or before any incremental save, never as the last file op of a long sequence on 4.3.2; `revert_file` keeps the doc's "refuse when never saved" rule. ACCEPTED 11:37. | Grace, evidence a1.crash.txt, REVCHK/REVCHK2/REVCHK3 |
| C16 | (B0-Q1) `PROTOCOL = 1` for 2.1.0: legacy add-ons are detected by the unknown-command reply, so 2.1.0 is simply the first declared protocol. Where Campbell's GV-1/GV-2 text reads "protocol 2", read 1 (Q6 in section 7 flags the conflict for confirmation). Bump rule (GV-1): PROTOCOL bumps when a handler key is renamed or removed, or an existing payload/reply key changes meaning or type; purely additive keys with addon-side defaults do not bump. | Lead 11:34 |
| C17 | (B0-Q2, amended 12:15 by C31) Session state: the server persists the JSON under `<settings dir>/sessions/<name>.json` and never reopens a file; the add-on applies the state and, on `restore_session_state`, reopens the file named in it through `load_blend`'s dirty guard (`force`) in ONE call. `get_session_state` / `apply_session_state` stay as the no-reopen primitives for callers that already hold the file. | Lead 11:34, 12:15 |
| C18 | (B0-Q3) Live steps L7-L9 go in the settings doc's own test plan; the MIGRATION doc gets a one-line pointer. | Lead 11:34 |
| C19 | Ton's measured facts and design (B0PROBE, both installs; accepted through D-e and the 11:34 ruling): (a) `bpy.app.timers` do NOT fire in `--background` while a script sleeps, so the autostart timer path is untestable headless; tests call `ensure_server()` directly, the timer path is live (L7). (b) `load_post/load_pre/save_pre/save_post` + `@persistent` on both; `exit_pre` 5.2.1 only, `hasattr`-guarded. (c) A `sys.path`-imported addon.py is NOT in `preferences.addons` after `register()` (KeyError on both): `_prefs()` returns None headless and every consumer falls back to defaults; the harness never gets prefs; `_migrate_legacy_secrets(prefs)` takes the prefs object as a parameter so tests can pass a `SimpleNamespace`. (d) `StringProperty(subtype='PASSWORD')` OK on both. (e) `'localhost'` resolves to `['127.0.0.1', '::1']`, the addon binds AF_INET: the addon binds `127.0.0.1` explicitly, the server default host becomes `127.0.0.1`; L3 step 1 verifies. Design D-a..D-g: `BLENDERMCP_AddonPreferences` gains `port` (1024-65535, default 9876), `autostart_server` (default True), four `PASSWORD` keys; module helpers `_prefs()`, `_port()`, `_secret(name)` (prefs value, else legacy scene value read-only); module-level `ensure_server(port=None) -> {running, port, host, started_now}` used by the deferred timer, the panel operator, the new handler `ensure_server_running` (wire `{}` -> `{"success": True, "running", "port", "host", "started_now"}`, "# Lifecycle" section next to `quit_blender`) and exposed as `bpy.app.driver_namespace["blendermcp_ensure_server"]` for `start_blender`'s `--python-expr`; `register()` schedules the deferred timer when `(_restart_flag_get() or prefs.autostart_server) and not bpy.app.background` (autostart never opens a port headless), the timer also runs the one-time legacy-secret migration; `Scene.blendermcp_server_running` dropped, `_server_running()` reads `bpy.types.blendermcp_server`; legacy Scene secret props stay REGISTERED but undrawn through 2.2.x, removed in 2.3.0 (D34 amended the earlier 2.2.0 date); `@persistent` `load_post` re-runs the migration and leaves the server alone; `exit_pre` stops the server (4.x: `unregister()` path). Headless `ensure_server()` tests use `port=0` (ephemeral) and stop the server in `finally`; the default port is never opened by a test. `_render_settings` becomes `with self._settings_scope(("RENDER","OUTPUT","CYCLES","EEVEE"))`; `_scope_owner`: RENDER=`scene.render`, OUTPUT=`scene.render.image_settings`, CYCLES=`scene.cycles`, EEVEE=`scene.eevee`, COLOR=`scene.view_settings` + `scene.display_settings`, VIEWPORT=VIEW_3D shading/overlay when a window exists, extras `scene.camera`, `scene.frame_current`; snapshot every writable non-pointer non-collection RNA property; restore order media_type before file_format, engine before engine-specific props; failed restores collected into the reply, not raised. Doc corrections: `use_nodes` exists on 5.2.1 as a no-op; the "All Libraries / Essentials at indices 0/1" claim is UNCONFIRMED headless (`asset_libraries == ['User Library']` on both), do not build on it. | Lead, Ton m_a72ca7a040ade852ca9a, m_05e75642f115c39de58f |
| C20 | Campbell's server design (B0-1..B0-5, GV-3; accepted): new module `src/blender_mcp/settings.py` with zero imports from `server.py` or `mcp`; API `load() -> dict` (defaults < `~/.blender_mcp/settings.json` < environment), `get(key)`, `update(values)` (writes the file, refreshes memory), `path()`; read once at import, a missing or corrupt file logs ONE warning and falls back, never raises; env overrides `BLENDER_HOST`, `BLENDER_PORT`, `BLENDER_EXE`, `IMG_TO_3D_PORT`, `BLENDER_MCP_NO_UPDATE_CHECK`, `BLENDER_MCP_UPDATE_REPO`, plus NEW `BLENDER_MCP_SETTINGS_DIR` so tests never touch the user's real file. Import-time consumers: `log_level` (basicConfig), `image_max_pixels` (getter), `img_to_3d_port` (the `_IMG_TO_3D_URL` f-string becomes `_img_to_3d_url()`; precedence env > file > 7862). Call-time consumers: host/port (defaults 127.0.0.1 / 9876), `command_timeout` (the two 180 s literals), NEW `connect_timeout` (connect has none today), `blender_exe` (resolver order hint > env > settings file > PATH > pool > Steam > macOS), `output_dir`, `presets_dir`. `set_server_settings` calls `update()` then `_drop_connection()` iff host or port changed. The addon never reads this file. Param styles unchanged: comma strings for lists, JSON strings for structured data with the `add_modifier` `set/unset` reply shape, tri-state `bool = None` for "= preference". | Lead, Campbell m_a2efbc13be3022a7aff7, m_d79c715ce2cc8b07d777 |
| C21 | Pre-existing server defect riding with B0 (accepted): `send_command`'s catch-all drops the socket on every handler status error. Fix: `BlenderCommandError` for `status == "error"` replies, excluded from the `sock = None` branch; wrappers then read `Error: <message>` (no "Communication error" prefix). Acceptance: mocked-transport unit test, socket object unchanged after a status error, dropped after a transport error. Also: `get_blender_status` probes `127.0.0.1` exactly like `BlenderConnection` (AF_INET); the dual-stack `create_connection(('localhost', ...))` walk is the suspected cause of "not reachable while LISTENING" and is verified in L3. | Lead, Campbell |
| C22 | `get_version` and the mismatch prefix (GV-1, GV-2; accepted; PROTOCOL value per C16): addon command `get_version` -> `{"addon": "2.1.0", "protocol": 1, "blender": bpy.app.version_string, "python": platform.python_version(), "addon_file": <addon.__file__>, "background": bpy.app.background}`. Legacy detection: a 2.0.0 add-on answers `Unknown command type: get_version`; the server maps that substring to "pre-2.1 add-on, protocol assumed"; any other exception = `unreachable (<reason>)`. Server tool `get_version` (no params, str, never touches GitHub): lines `BlenderMCP server <ver> (protocol <n>)`, `Server module: <path>`, `Settings file: <path> (loaded | not created yet)`, `Blender add-on: <ver> (protocol <n>) on Blender <ver>, Python <ver>, file <addon_file> | unreachable (<reason>) | pre-2.1 (no get_version handler)`, `Compatibility: OK | MISMATCH. <prefix text>` (always present), `Update check: not run yet | disabled (BLENDER_MCP_NO_UPDATE_CHECK) | ...` (the GitHub check itself is B8+). Placement: `PROTOCOL` module constant directly under `bl_info` in addon.py; `__version__` then `PROTOCOL` in `src/blender_mcp/__init__.py` ABOVE the `from .server import ...` line; `server.py` does `from . import __version__, PROTOCOL`, no literal copies; `tests/test_version_sync.py` also asserts the two PROTOCOLs equal. Mismatch mechanism: `_probe_addon_version(conn)` in `get_blender_connection()` after `connect()`, beside the polyhaven probe, stores `_addon_info`; on version or protocol mismatch sets `_pending_notices["mismatch"]` = `[blender-mcp mismatch] Server <ver> / protocol <n> but the Blender add-on reports <ver> / protocol <n>. Wire formats differ; deploy addon.py and cycle the add-on before continuing.`; re-runs on every reconnect. Emission: one wrapper around `mcp.tool` installed after `mcp = FastMCP(...)` and before the first `@mcp.tool()` (`functools.wraps`, sync and async branches); `_attach_notice(result)` prepends `<notices>\n---\n` to the next `str` reply only, images untouched; once per session via `_mismatch_notified`, re-armed only after the versions are seen agreeing again; no lock needed (single event loop, no await in `_attach_notice`). Acceptance: tools/list dump byte-identical before/after the wrapper. | Lead, Campbell m_f5e22bf111b5482c9f10, m_c4aa054c8d3b592a27c9 |

All on both. Names per profile as the doc says. | `collision` |
| E12 | Names: data-block name limit is 63 bytes on 4.3.2 and 255 on 5.2.1; collisions suffix `.001/.002` on both. `validate_asset` name checks use the live limit (`len(obj.name)` after assignment, never a constant). | `misc.max_name_len` |
| E13 | Numbers: `get_triangle_budget` / `get_mesh_stats.triangles` use `evaluated_get(depsgraph).data.calc_loop_triangles()` (6 polys + subsurf 2 -> 192 tris on both). `compare_meshes` first pass = `mesh.unit_test_compare(mesh=, threshold=)` ("Same" or a difference string whose wording differs per version: match on `== "Same"` only); numpy pass uses `foreach_get` into preallocated `float64` buffers (M12; `numpy.array(Vector)` is float32 on 5.2.1). `set_origin(BOTTOM_CENTER)` = `mesh.transform(Matrix.Translation)` + `location` offset (verified). | `misc` |
| E14 | Timbermesh: the plugin module is present on 4.3.2 (user add-ons folder) and ABSENT on 5.2.1 (not installed). `export_for_engine(TIMBERMESH)` errors with a teach-style message when `addon_utils` cannot find the module; its exporter path stays as the doc describes and is verified live on 4.3.2 only until the user installs it on 5.2 (L-step). C13 applies to its enable call (`default_set=True`). | `misc.timbermesh_module`; gdcheck `addons_all` |

### 12.0 House rules (team nosubl, from the Lead's brief, verbatim)

ACK every assignment, finding, ruling and gate signal by DM within one poll; no ACK in 5 min =
re-assigned; one file = one owner; contract changes only via the Planner as a versioned
amendment (v1.1, v1.2, ...) ratified by the Lead; every deviation declared even when trivial;
peer agreements need Lead ratification.

Standing rulings carried over from section 0 and the 2026-09-11 run (not reopened): nobody
commits, deploys, launches a Blender GUI or the live MCP server; live copies (`B:\...`,
`D:\blender-4.3.2-windows-x64\MCP\`, `%APPDATA%` add-ons) are never edited; headless command is
`<install>\blender.exe --background --factory-startup --python <script>`; evidence = pasted
output, PASS/FAIL per step, `git hash-object <file>` for every landing, in the same minute as
the landing; 4.x/5.x differences by try-assign or hasattr, NEVER `bpy.app.version` compares
(M7 grep stays 0); assertions are never weakened; everything scripted prints ASCII only.
Progress, hashes and test output go on the owner's kanban card (board `team-nosubl`, db
`creative-suite`), the room carries questions, rulings and one-line signals.

Lead rulings R1-R10 are the file `docs/LEAD-RULINGS-C.md` (owner Lead; by reference per the Lead's 22:17Z
ruling, `git hash-object` of that file is posted on card d1-p at each amendment). R7 and R8 verbatim:

- **R7 COMMS.** Inbox first on every wake; ACK every assignment / finding / ruling / gate signal
  by DM within one poll; no ACK in 5 min = re-assigned. Findings as finding / evidence / who it
  blocks. Room posts <= 2500 chars; long material goes on your kanban card (board `team-nosubl`,
  db `creative-suite`; card ids are lowercase: `c-recon-addon`, `c-recon-server`, `c-recon-qa`,
  slices `c-a-l1` ...) or on disk with the path posted.
- **R8 CONTRACT.** Coders build only from a PLAN.md version the Lead has ratified in the room.
  Changes only as versioned amendments through Barbara (C.1, C.2, ...), re-posted as diffs.

Lead rulings R11-R21 (room posts 22:10Z and 22:12Z, verbatim):

- R11 tools/list baseline: tests/test_f6_connection.py `test_tools_list_byte_identical_to_2_2_baseline` goes red by design at 2.3.0. Margaret repoints it at C-S0: names == tests/baseline_tools_2.3.txt + schema == tests/baselines/tools_list_2.3.json (both written from the real list via tests/dump_tools_list.py), PLUS a superset assertion that every 2.2 name keeps its 2.2 schema byte-identical (pattern test_b0_server.py:369). The 2.2 baseline files stay on disk untouched.
- R12 JSON params on the server side: object params -> `_parse_json_object` (server.py:3412); list-or-object params (rna_set sets, batch commands, call_operator params/context, call_method args/kwargs) -> `_parse_json_any` (:5733); "comma list or JSON list" params (rna_get refs, data_remove refs, scene_snapshot types/fields) -> NEW helper `_parse_refs(text, label)` in server.py (Grace): JSON list when text.lstrip() starts with "[", else `_split_csv`. Error texts are the existing ones verbatim ("Error: <label> is not valid JSON (...)", "Error: <label> must be a JSON object, ..."); the contract and test_drv_server.py quote them. All params stay typed str (FastMCP schema).
- R13 settings keys: settings.py DEFAULTS += driver_reply_max_chars 24000, driver_batch_max 200; ENV_KEYS += BLENDER_DRIVER_REPLY_MAX, BLENDER_DRIVER_BATCH_MAX; read at use time via int(_settings.get(...)) like _img_to_3d_port (server.py:1844). Docstrings/TOOLS/README key lists updated by Grace in the docs slice.
- R14 test_drv_server.py copies the FakeConn + server_in block from test_b0_server.py:25-66 verbatim (house idiom: no conftest, no cross-test import).
- R15 settings byte-identity gate = `tests\settings_baseline.py -- --check` match:true on both installs (Margaret's fixtures at addon 0eb7ec4c, frozen).
- R16 UID lookup: NO index/cache. `_resolve_ref("#uid")` does a full bpy.data scan per call (measured worst 0.27 ms on 2000 objects); on 5.x skip `all_ids` (double counts). Uids are monotonic, never reused, survive rename and undo.
- R17 Undo WORKS in --background on both installs: `transaction` ROLLBACK and `batch(undo=AUTO)` are asserted for real in the harness (steps 7/8 assert location restored), not the teaching message.
- R18 msgbus is unprovable headless (no delivery while a --background script runs): `rna_set(publish=True)` stays (cheap, correct live) but no harness step asserts msgbus delivery; Tier 2 `property` events are live-only; depsgraph/frame events (which DO fire headless) are the testable path.
- R19 `temp_override` accepts ANY key silently, so `_apply_context` whitelists: window, screen, area, region, scene, view_layer, object, active_object, selected_objects, selected_editable_objects, edit_object, pose_object, active_bone, active_pose_bone, modifier, point_cache. Unknown key -> teaching error listing the whitelist. `bl_options` is unreachable for C operators: DROP it from the describe(operator) reply.
- R20 Headless has a window, a screen and 4 areas under --factory-startup (window_none_headless=false): the "needs the GUI" error is keyed on `bpy.app.background`, never on window/area being None; `context_info` reports both `window: bool` and `background: bool`.
- R21 Caps stand: driver_reply_max_chars 24000 (a flat Object dump is 4.5-4.8 KB), driver_batch_max 200. RNA error texts are identical on both installs and are passed through verbatim inside the teaching error (`rna_error` key), never re-worded.

Lead rulings R22-R28 (DM 22:18Z, verbatim; decisive facts measured on both installs by Linus, card c-recon-addon):

- R22 `transaction` by Scene marker: BEGIN = `undo_push("mcp:<name>:pre")`, `scene["_mcp_txn"] = seq`, `undo_push("mcp:<name>")`; ROLLBACK = `ed.undo` while `scene.get("_mcp_txn") == seq` (cap = undo_steps); COMMIT deletes the idprop. Reply keys `undo_calls`, `marker_present`, `undo_available` replace `pushes`. Declared side effects: one Scene idprop, `is_dirty`.
- R23 rna_get depth=0 default INCLUDES pointers as ref dicts (amend the 12.2.2 row + 12.2.4).
- R24 reuse reading of the settings core, bodies untouched.
- R25 execute_code returns the error dict, never raises.
- R26 G5 derived map is OUT: static id_type -> collection table with ordered hasattr candidates (GreasePencil struct name moved between 4.3 and 5.2, so fixed_type.identifier derivation gives the wrong collection on one version); unknown IDTYPE -> {error, valid}.
- R27 batch wall cap checked between commands (declared deviation).
- R28 add_modifier keeps plain object names for pointer props.
- R29 2.2 superset assertion = every 2.2 tool keeps every 2.2 inputSchema property with the same type and unchanged `required`; additive properties and description changes are allowed. Byte-identity only against the 2.3 baselines.
- Also: call_operator.reports = redirect_stdout lines (G10 confirmed headless: "Info: Saved ..." captured).

Lead rulings R30-R39 and oracle rulings O1-O4, VERBATIM from `docs/LEAD-RULINGS-C.md` (hash 4c8a9c66, owner Lead):

- **R30** A batch item is "error" when its result dict has an `error` key OR `success: false` (an rna_set whose items all failed trips stop_on_error).
- **R31** `_parse_json_any` refuses any decoded scalar (str/int/float/bool/null) with `Error: <label> must be a JSON object or list`; the fix is at the chokepoint so all 11 callers (incl. the 2.2 rigging wrappers, which echoed a JSON string value as the reply) inherit it; QA proves the class closed with a case at a 2.2 call site that is red against the committed 2.2.0 server.
- **R32 / R32b** `drv_describe` truncation order at the cap: (1) blank property `description` strings and the description element of `enum_items` (3-element shape kept), (2) only then drop whole properties from the END, named compactly. The default cap stays 24000 (measured: describe("Object") = 38965 chars full, 30201 with descriptions blanked, so ~30 trailing properties still drop); `count` = kept entries, `total` = declared; the "130+ properties" assertion lives on the `max_chars=60000` variant.
- **R33** The depth walk (rna_get, scene_snapshot, find expansion) never dereferences pointers on UNSAFE structs: `_DRV_UNSAFE_STRUCTS = {ToolSettings, Paint (and any struct whose bl_rna.base chain reaches Paint), CurveMapping}`; such pointers are reported at depth-0 shape only and the skip is named once in `truncated`. Cause: `scene.tool_settings.uv_sculpt` is an uninitialised Paint struct in the 4.3.2 factory file; a plain getattr faults the process (frames rna_Paint_eraser_brush_get, brush_asset_reference.asset_library_type, CurveMapping_tone_get). A run without a SUMMARY on one build is a landing FAIL, never evidence.
- **R34** `truncated` entries are compact ("properties: 30 of 132 dropped, e.g. ...; narrow with fields= or raise max_chars="); the envelope stays <= 400 chars and the cap check counts the final JSON including `truncated`.
- **R35** A ref ending in "/" with an empty path is refused for every root (`context/` like `OBJECT:Cube/`); bare `context`, `data`, `prefs` resolve to the root struct.
- **R36** The planned "coercer read-only skip" mutant cannot discriminate (RNA refuses the assignment itself); the add-on discriminators are mutant C (default cap off -> DRV-1/DRV-9 FAIL), D (DEGREES conversion off -> DRV-3 FAIL), E (context whitelist off -> DRV-5 FAIL), F (R30 item rule off -> DRV-8 FAIL), G (`_DRV_BATCH_MAX` off -> 201 refusal FAIL); QA ratifies survival independently on scratch copies.
- **R37** `call_operator` validates `params` against the operator's RNA BEFORE poll (refused-op check first, params second, poll third), so a misspelt param returns `{error, closest}` even in the wrong context.
- **R38** L4 events (`events_subscribe` / `events_poll` / `events_unsubscribe`) are OUT of 2.3.0: 13 driver tools, 182 total. msgbus delivery is unprovable headless; depsgraph/frame events need `@persistent` handlers in `register()`; nothing in Tier 1 depends on them. First item of 2.4.0 with the R18 facts attached.
- **R39** (F12) A wall-capped batch counts the wall stop as an error for the rollback decision: reply `success: false`, `rolled_back` per the undo mode (AUTO + stop_on_error rolls back the completed commands), `stopped_at` = first command not run, plus `error` = `wall cap <N> s reached after command <i>; <k> skipped`.
- **Oracle rulings O1-O4**: O1 = R32b supersedes "truncated == []" at the default cap; O2 = the R33 skip note is present even uncapped; O3 = `get_version.registries` lists only the `blendermcp_*` driver_namespace registries that exist (no pre-creation); O4 = the INVOKE refusal < 50 ms stands as written.

Mutant H (Linus, L3.1) = F12 reintroduced -> DRV-8 FAIL, discriminating. R33 also rules the enum_items description element blanked at stage 1 and the third crash frame
`brush_asset_reference.asset_library_type`; G21 below carries the same set.

Lead deltas on D1.0 (DM 22:15Z), folded in below: (1) D-G0 is done, 12.6 filled from disk; (2) G4 -> R16;
(3) G8 -> R17; (4) 12.2.3 extra whitelist, drop bl_options, GUI error on background (R19/R20); (5) G6 -> R12,
fixture from test_b0_server.py:25-66 (R14); (6) settings re-implementation DEFERRED (debt), R15 is a D-G3
regression check, `add_modifier.props` through `_coerce_value` IN as Linus L3; (7) G13 accepted;
(8) R11 and R21 into 12.1.

### 12.1 Decisions (the decisive fact per row)

| # | Decision | Decisive fact |
|---|---|---|
| G1 (R1, Q1 = A) | Scope = the request doc's Tier 1 (11 tools) PLUS `find` and `context_info` from Tier 2, as 2.3.0; request-doc steps 2 and 5 (which use `find` / `context_info`) are therefore BINDING for the Tier 1 gate (I1); step 10 (events) is L4-only. Events (`events_subscribe` / `events_poll` / `events_unsubscribe`) are OUT of 2.3.0 (R38, ruled 23:09Z): first item of 2.4.0 (12.9). | `find` and `context_info` are pure reads over existing primitives (verified in `cdp.py`); events need `@persistent` handlers in `register()`, a ring buffer and D-G0 facts 3-4 (12.6), so they are the only part that can slip without breaking the rest. 13 tools: 169 -> 182 FINAL (R38). |
| G2 | PROTOCOL stays 1. New handler keys `drv_*` only; every change to an existing reply is an added key. | Section 0 rule: additive keys never bump. `tests/baseline_tools_2.2.txt` stays a strict subset of the new list (K14 pattern). |
| G3 | The driver REUSES the settings-phase RNA helpers instead of adding a second coercer: `_rna_get` (addon.py:2305) is the scalar encoder, `_rna_set_validated` (2318) the scalar validator/setter, `_prop_default` (4216) the default reader. New helpers wrap them: `_encode_value` adds pointers / collections / matrices / depth; `_coerce_value` adds pointer-by-ref, angle conversion and ID-property paths. The driver CALLS these helpers; their bodies are not edited (R24), which is what keeps R15 green. | Both helpers already carry the enum / read-only / range / dynamic-enum rules the request doc asks for; a parallel implementation would drift from `set_settings`. |
| G4 (R16) | Uid lookup = `_uid_lookup(uid)`: a full `bpy.data` scan per call over every COLLECTION property of `bpy.data.bl_rna` EXCEPT `all_ids` (5.x union view, double counts); no cache, no driver_namespace key, no `_on_load_post` change. Modifiers, bones, constraints and nodes have no uid (`ids.modifier_has_uid` false): ID + path refs only. | Measured (DRVCHECK): worst scan 0.243 ms (4.3.2) / 0.265 ms (5.2.1) on 2000 objects; uids monotonic, never reused after remove, survive rename and undo. A cache would be code without a measurable benefit. |
| G5 (R26) | `IDTYPE:` in a ref maps to a `bpy.data` collection through a STATIC table `_ID_COLLECTIONS = {id_type: (candidate, candidate, ...)}` resolved with ordered `hasattr(bpy.data, candidate)` at call time (e.g. `GREASEPENCIL: ("grease_pencils_v3", "grease_pencils")` on 4.3 vs `("grease_pencils",)` on 5.2 by the same ordered tuple). Unknown IDTYPE -> `{error, valid: [the table's keys]}`. The derived map of D1.0/D1.1 is OUT. | Linus measured that the GreasePencil struct name moved between 4.3 and 5.2, so deriving the collection from `fixed_type.identifier` picks the wrong collection on one version. The `idtype_map` drvcheck step is informational only. |
| G6 (R12) | JSON-typed parameters cross the MCP boundary as strings (FastMCP schema) and are parsed SERVER-side with the EXISTING helpers: object params -> `_parse_json_object` (server.py:3412); list-or-object params (`sets`, `commands`, `params`, `context`, `args`, `kwargs`, `where`, `watch`) -> `_parse_json_any` (:5733); comma-or-JSON-list params (`refs`, `types`, `fields`, `include`, `kinds`) -> NEW `_parse_refs(text, label)` (JSON list when `text.lstrip()` starts with `[`, else `_split_csv`; `None`/empty -> `[]`; a non-string item -> `Error: <label> must be a JSON list of strings, e.g. '["#127", "OBJECT:Cube"]'`). Parse BEFORE the try/`get_blender_connection` block and return the `Error: ...` string with ZERO wire calls (idiom: `set_settings` :4045, `add_bones` :5814). No `_parse_json`. Canonical wire shapes (I2): `refs` list[str]; `sets` list of `[ref, value]` pairs (a JSON object is normalised to pairs by the SERVER); `commands` list[dict]; `params`/`context`/`kwargs`/`set` dict; `args` list; `where` list[list]. The add-on also accepts strings through `_json_arg` (addon.py:3828) and a dict for `sets`, so the harness may call either shape. | The repo convention since B0/B1; one canonical shape per param is what `test_drv_server.py` S2 asserts. |
| G7 (R32, R34) | Reply cap: the server ALWAYS sends `max_chars` (the tool's `max_chars` or the settings key `driver_reply_max_chars`, default 24000) on every reading command; the add-on applies the cap while building the reply and defaults to 24000 when the key is missing (old server). Batch size: the server refuses more than `driver_batch_max` (default 200) commands BEFORE any `send_command`; the add-on refuses more than 200 as well and stops at 60 s wall time. | The add-on builds the reply, so the cap must be applied there; the server owns settings, so the default lives there. ### 12.2 Interface contract (v D1.1)

#### 12.2.1 The `ref` grammar (every driver command; no driver tool takes `object_name`)

| Form | Resolves to | Example |
|---|---|---|
| `#<uid>` | the ID with that `session_uid` (G4) | `#127` |
| `<IDTYPE>:<name>` | `bpy.data.<collection>[name]` via G5; IDTYPE case-insensitive | `OBJECT:Cube`, `mesh:Cube`, `NODETREE:Geometry Nodes` |
| `<id ref>/<path>` | an RNA path under that ID in `path_resolve` syntax | `OBJECT:Cube/modifiers["Bevel"].width`, `#127/location[2]`, `#127/["my_prop"]` |
| `context` / `context/<path>` | `bpy.context` as root (`context/scene.frame_current`, `context/active_object`) | |
| `data` / `data/<path>` | `bpy.data` as root (`data/objects`, `data/materials["Red"]`) | |
| `prefs` / `prefs/<path>` | `bpy.context.preferences` as root | `prefs/edit.undo_steps` |

Resolution (`_resolve_ref(ref) -> (owner, leaf, index)`): the part before the first `/` is the
root; the path is split at the LAST `.` that is outside brackets into `parent_path` and `leaf`;
a trailing `[<int>]` on the leaf is `index`, a leaf of the form `["key"]` alone is an
ID-property access. `owner = root.path_resolve(parent_path, False)` when `parent_path` is not
empty. A ref ending on a struct (no leaf, or the leaf is a POINTER/COLLECTION) resolves to that
struct with `leaf=None`. Failure -> `{"error": "<what failed>", "tried": [<collection or path
searched>], "closest": [<=3 difflib matches when a property or IDTYPE was misspelt>]}`.
A trailing `/` is malformed (R35): `OBJECT:Cube/` and `context/` are errors, never silently stripped.
Every reply that mentions an ID carries `{"uid": int, "id_type": str, "name": str}`.

#### 12.2.2 Handler table (wire keys; the server sends EXACTLY these parameter names)

Every handler returns a dict. Success replies carry `"success": true`; reading replies also
carry `"chars": <len of the json reply>` and `"truncated": [<what was dropped and which
filter narrows it>]` (empty list when nothing was dropped). Error replies carry `"error": str`
and, where the row says so, the teaching keys `closest`, `valid`, `tried`, `poll_message`,
`context_applied`, `undo_available`. Handlers never raise for a bad input (the harness `call`
helper treats `{"status": "error"}` as a FAIL for driver steps).

| Tool (server) | Handler key | Handler kwargs (defaults) | Reply |
|---|---|---|---|
| `describe` | `drv_describe` | `what: str`, `fields="all"`, `include_functions=False`, `search=None`, `max_chars=None` | struct: `{success, kind: "struct", identifier, base, description, count, properties: {id: {type, subtype, unit, readonly, array_length, default, min, max, soft_min, soft_max, enum_items: [[id, name, description]], dynamic: bool (ENUM with empty static items), fixed_type, is_animatable}}, functions?: {name: {description, params: [[id, type, is_output, is_required]]}}, chars, truncated}`; operator: `{success, kind: "operator", idname, name, description, properties (same shape), poll_now: bool, refused: bool}` (no `bl_options`: unreachable for C operators, R19); `what="ops"` + `search`: `{success, kind: "ops", count, total, operators: [[idname, description]]}`. Not found -> `{error, closest}` over struct names (`dir(bpy.types)`) or operator ids. Truncation (R32/R32b/R34): descriptions dropped first (one compact `truncated` line), then trailing properties; `count` = entries present, `total` = declared; never a half-null entry; unsafe structs (R33) are skipped and named in `truncated`. |
| `rna_get` | `drv_get` | `refs: list[str]`, `fields=None`, `depth=0`, `angles="RADIANS"`, `max_chars=None` | `{success, results: [{ref, uid?, id_type?, name?, value}], errors: [{ref, error, tried?, closest?}], chars, truncated}`. `fields` default for a struct = every property except COLLECTIONs, POINTERs INCLUDED as ref dicts (`{uid, id_type, name}` for IDs, `{struct, path}` for non-ID structs) at `depth=0` (R23); `"all"` = everything to `depth`; a collection named with `[]` (e.g. `modifiers[]`) lists items. One round trip for many refs; a bad ref lands in `errors`, the others still read. |
| `rna_set` | `drv_set` | `sets: list[[ref, value]] or dict{ref: value}`, `angles="RADIANS"`, `stop_on_error=True`, `publish=True` | `{success, results: [{ref, set: bool, value_after?, error?, valid?, closest?, rna_error?}], applied: int, failed: int, stopped_at: int or null}`. `success` is true when `failed == 0`. Side-effect props go through their API (`frame_current` -> `scene.frame_set`); the map `_DRV_SETTERS` starts with that one entry. |
| `call_operator` | `drv_op` | `op: str`, `params=None (dict)`, `context=None (dict, 12.2.3)`, `exec_context="EXEC_DEFAULT"`, `undo=True` | ok: `{success, result: [str], reports: [str], context_applied: {...}, elapsed_ms, modal: bool}`; poll failure: `{error, poll_message, context_applied}`; bad kwarg: `{error, closest, rna_error}` and bad enum kwarg: `{error, valid, rna_error}`, both evaluated BEFORE poll (R37); unknown `context.extra` key: `{error, valid: [the 16 whitelisted keys]}`; refused op: `{error: "...refused...", refused: true}`; GUI-only context when `bpy.app.background`: `{error: "Viewport context needs a GUI session: ...", background: true}` (same wording family as the capture tools; never keyed on window None, R20). |
| `call_method` | `drv_call` | `ref: str`, `method: str`, `args=None (list)`, `kwargs=None (dict)`, `depsgraph=False`, `max_chars=None` | `{success, value, chars, truncated}`; an ID return value is `{uid, id_type, name}`, a struct return `{struct, path}` when `path_from_id` works. Unknown method -> `{error, closest}` over `bl_rna.functions`. |
| `data_new` | `drv_new` | `collection: str`, `args=None (list)`, `kwargs=None (dict)`, `link_to=None`, `set=None (list or dict as rna_set)` | `{success, uid, id_type, name, linked_to: str or null, set: {applied, failed, results}}`. `link_to` default for `objects` = the active scene's root collection; `"scene"`, `"COLLECTION:Name"` or `"#uid"` accepted; non-object IDs are never linked (`linked_to: null`). No `new` on the collection -> `{error, valid: [collections with new]}`. |
| `data_remove` | `drv_remove` | `refs: list[str]`, `do_unlink=True`, `purge_orphans=False` | `{success, results: [{ref, removed: bool, users_before: int, error?}], purged: int or null}`. A ref with users and `do_unlink=False` is refused per item with `users_before`. |
| `scene_snapshot` | `drv_snapshot` | `name: str`, `scope="SCENE"`, `types=None (list[str])`, `fields="name,type,parent,location,rotation_euler,scale,hide_viewport,hide_render,users"`, `include=None (list[str] of modifiers, constraints, material_slots, vertex_groups, children)`, `depth=1`, `return_tree=True`, `angles="RADIANS"`, `max_chars=None` | `{success, name, captured_at, scope, count, ids: {uid: {id_type, name, <fields present on that ID>, <include lists>}}, tree: [{uid, children: [...]}], chars, truncated}`; `return_tree=False` -> `{success, name, count, chars}`. A field an ID does not have is omitted, never an error. Stored in `driver_namespace["blendermcp_driver_snapshots"][name] = {params, data}`. |
| `scene_diff` | `drv_diff` | `a: str`, `b="NOW"`, `max_chars=None` | `{success, a, b, added: [{uid, id_type, name}], removed: [...], renamed: [{uid, before, after}], changed: {uid: {field: [before, after]}}, chars, truncated}`; a renamed ID appears in `renamed` only, `name` is never a `changed` field (G19). `b="NOW"` captures the live scene with `a`'s stored params. Unknown name -> `{error, valid: [snapshot names]}`. |
| `batch` | `drv_batch` | `commands: list[{id?: str, type: str, params?: dict}]`, `stop_on_error=True`, `undo="AUTO"` or `"NONE"`, `refs=True`, `max_chars=None` | `{success, results: [{id, type, status: "ok"/"error"/"skipped", result?, error?, elapsed_ms}], rolled_back: bool, undo_available: bool, stopped_at: int or null, elapsed_ms, chars, truncated}`. `success` = no command errored. `"$<id>.<path>"` strings in later params are replaced from earlier results (`path` = dotted keys and `[index]`); a missing id or path is that command's error. `drv_batch` as a command type -> `{error: "nested batch refused"}`; > 200 commands -> refused before running anything; > 60 s wall, checked between commands (R27) -> stop, remaining `skipped`, `success: false`, `error: "wall cap <N> s reached after command <i>; <k> skipped"`, `stopped_at` = first command not run, rollback when `undo="AUTO"` and `stop_on_error` (R39); `rolled_back` uses the `_mcp_batch` Scene marker (G8). |
| `transaction` | `drv_transaction` | `action: "BEGIN"/"COMMIT"/"ROLLBACK"/"STATUS"`, `name=None` | `{success, action, name, undo_calls: int, marker_present: bool, undo_available: bool, open: bool}`; nested `BEGIN` -> `{error: "transaction '<open>' is open; COMMIT or ROLLBACK it first", open: true, name}`; ROLLBACK without undo -> the G8 error; ROLLBACK that hits the `undo_steps` cap with the marker still present -> `{error: "marker still present after N undo steps", undo_calls: N, marker_present: true}`. Marker = Scene idprop `_mcp_txn` holding `seq` (R22); name and seq are mirrored in `driver_namespace["blendermcp_driver_txn"]` for STATUS. |
| `find` | `drv_find` | `id_type="OBJECT"`, `name=None (glob, fnmatch)`, `where=None (list[[path, op, value]])`, `scope="SCENE"`, `fields="name,type"`, `limit=100`, `max_chars=None` | `{success, count, total, items: [{uid, id_type, name, <fields>}], chars, truncated}`. `op` in `==, !=, <, <=, >, >=, in, contains, exists`; a path with `[]` means "any item" (`["modifiers[].type", "contains", "ARMATURE"]`). `scope="SCENE"` is honoured for OBJECT (`scene.objects`); other id types always search `bpy.data` and the reply says `scope: "DATA"`. Bad op -> `{error, valid}`. |
| `context_info` | `drv_context` | `fields=None` | `{success, mode, active_object: {uid,...} or null, selected: [{uid,...}], scene, view_layer, window: bool, background: bool, areas: [{type, regions: [str]}], frame_current, edit_object, pose_object}`. Headless under `--factory-startup` reports `window: true, areas: 4, background: true` (DRVCHECK ctx). |
| OUT (R38, 2.4.0) `events_subscribe` | `drv_sub` | `kinds: list[str] from depsgraph, frame, render, bake, file, undo, property`, `watch=None (list[[struct, prop]])`, `buffer=1000` | `{success, subscription: str, cursor: int, kinds}` |
| OUT (R38, 2.4.0) `events_poll` | `drv_poll` | `cursor: int`, `limit=200`, `kinds=None` | `{success, events: [{seq, t, kind, data}], cursor, dropped}` (the server does the `wait_ms` sleep-and-retry in <= 250 ms steps; the add-on never blocks) |
| OUT (R38, 2.4.0) `events_unsubscribe` | `drv_unsub` | `subscription=None` | `{success, cleared: [str]}` |

#### 12.2.3 The `context` declaration (`call_operator`)

`{"mode": "EDIT", "active": "#127", "selected": ["#127", "OBJECT:Cutter"], "area": "VIEW_3D",
"region": "WINDOW", "scene": "SCENE:Scene", "keep": false, "extra": {"edit_object": "#127"}}`.
`active` / `selected` -> `_selection_scope` (addon.py:837); `mode` -> `object.mode_set` inside
`_mode_restore` (addon.py:882); `area` / `region` -> first matching area of `_gui_window()`
(addon.py:4840) under `temp_override(window, area, region)`, the capture-tool idiom; headless
-> the GUI-only error when `bpy.app.background` (R20); `extra` keys are WHITELISTED (R19: `temp_override`
accepts any key silently): `window, screen, area, region, scene, view_layer, object, active_object,
selected_objects, selected_editable_objects, edit_object, pose_object, active_bone, active_pose_bone,
modifier, point_cache`; an unknown key -> `{error, valid: [the whitelist]}` before anything runs;
`keep=true` skips the restores. `context_applied` echoes what was applied
as `{mode, active: uid, selected: [uid], area, region, extra_keys}`.

#### 12.2.4 Value encoding (`_encode_value`, used by rna_get, call_method, snapshot, batch, execute_code value)

| RNA | JSON |
|---|---|
| INT / FLOAT / BOOLEAN scalar | number / bool |
| arrays (vectors, colors, bool arrays) | list; `unit == 'ROTATION'` scaled when `angles="DEGREES"` |
| matrices | list of row lists |
| STRING | string |
| ENUM | identifier string; enum flags -> sorted list of identifiers |
| POINTER to an ID | `{uid, id_type, name}` or `null` |
| POINTER to a non-ID struct | `{struct: <identifier>, path: <path_from_id or null>}` at depth 0; expanded to `depth`. Pointers are part of the DEFAULT field set (R23). |
| COLLECTION | `{count}`; `{count, items: [...]}` when named with `[]` in `fields` or `fields="all"` with `depth > 0` |
| ID properties | under `custom: {}` (JSON as stored) |
| anything else | `str(value)` with `"encoded": "str"` on the entry |

Coercion on the way in (`_coerce_value(prop, value, angles)`): list -> vector/color/matrix;
string -> enum (validated, `valid` lists the identifiers); `"#uid"`, `"IDTYPE:Name"` or
`{"uid": n}` -> pointer resolved against `prop.fixed_type`; int -> float; 0/1 -> bool;
`["key"]` ID-property paths accept any JSON value; read-only -> refused with the RNA message.

#### 12.2.5 Settings (server) and constants (add-on)

`settings.py DEFAULTS` gains `"driver_reply_max_chars": 24000` and `"driver_batch_max": 200`;
`ENV_KEYS` gains `BLENDER_DRIVER_REPLY_MAX` and `BLENDER_DRIVER_BATCH_MAX`. The add-on holds
`_DRV_REPLY_MAX_DEFAULT = 24000`, `_DRV_BATCH_MAX = 200`, `_DRV_BATCH_WALL_S = 60.0`,
`_DRV_REFUSED_OPS = frozenset({"wm.quit_blender", "wm.console_toggle", "wm.window_new",
"wm.window_fullscreen_toggle", "screen.animation_play", "wm.read_factory_settings",
"preferences.addon_disable", "script.reload"})` (`preferences.addon_disable` refused only
when `module` names this add-on). All four are MODULE-level (G19) so the harness can lower the caps. Also module-level: `_DRV_UNSAFE_STRUCTS = frozenset({"ToolSettings", "Paint", "CurveMapping"})` and the class constant `_DRV_UNSAFE_PROPS = frozenset({"uv_sculpt"})` (R33).
Settings are read at use time via `int(_settings.get("driver_reply_max_chars", 24000))`, the
`_img_to_3d_port` idiom (server.py:1844) (R13). Reply-size trace: 24000 chars default cap, every reading
reply <= `max_chars` + the envelope (`success`, `chars`, `truncated`, <= 400 chars).

#### 12.2.6 Test-facing symbols (contract; a rename is an amendment)

- Handler keys: the 13 (+3) `drv_*` keys above, present in `_build_handlers()`'s
  `extended_handlers` under a `# Generic driver (D1)` comment.
- Add-on helpers (private, but the harness may call them for fixture checks): `_resolve_ref` (raises
  `_DrvRefError` whose `.reply` is the teaching dict; every `drv_*` is wrapped by `@_drv_handler` and never
  raises), `_split_path`, `_encode_value`, `_coerce_value(prop, value, angles) -> (value, error)`,
  `_drv_assign`, `_describe_struct`, `_describe_op`, `_apply_context`, `_uid_lookup(uid)`,
  `_ID_COLLECTIONS` (static table, R26), `_drv_mark` / `_drv_unwind` (R22); module constants per 12.2.5.
  (Linus's declared deviations (1)-(13) of L1, (1)-(9) of L2 and (1)-(5) of L3 on card d1-a are accepted
  and part of the contract.)
- driver_namespace keys: `blendermcp_driver_snapshots`, `blendermcp_driver_txn`, L4
  `blendermcp_driver_events`. No uid key (R16).
- Server: tool names as in 12.2.2; `_parse_refs(text, label)` (R12) next to `_parse_json_object`
  / `_parse_json_any` / `_split_csv`; `_driver_cap(max_chars)` returning the int to send; the
  batch refusal text starts with `"Error: batch has N commands; the limit is M"` and is returned
  with ZERO `send_command` calls. Error texts for bad JSON are the existing ones verbatim:
  `Error: <label> is not valid JSON (<msg> at position N)`, `Error: <label> must be a JSON object, e.g. '{"key": value}'`.
- Tests: `tests/drv_steps.py` loaded by `exec(compile(open(path).read(), path, "exec"))` behind
  `-- --drv` exactly like `b1_steps.py` (headless_handlers.py:1707-1711), sharing `step`, `call`,
  `ok`, `err`, `make_cube`, `clear_scene`, `WORK`, `srv`, `HANDLERS`, `P`, `A`; `DRV_TABLE` in
  `tests/headless_handlers.py`; `tests/test_drv_server.py` with the `FakeConn` class + `server_in`
  fixture copied VERBATIM from `tests/test_b0_server.py:25-66` (R14); `tests/baseline_tools_2.3.txt`
  + `tests/baselines/tools_list_2.3.json` cut after D-G3; `tests/settings_baseline.py -- --check`
  (R15) is the settings regression check.
- Harness invariants (`step()` at headless_handlers.py:161) hold for every driver step: mode
  back to OBJECT, no leaked scenes.

How the server slice proceeds before the add-on lands: Grace builds against 12.2.2 with
`FakeConn` replies shaped per the table; nothing in `server.py` reads an add-on reply key that
is not in 12.2.2. The seam is the handler table; the first end-to-end proof is D-G1 + D-G2 on
`describe("Object")` -> `data_new` -> `rna_set` -> `rna_get` (request doc steps 1-3).

### 12.5 Risks and seams (each with the test that fails when it happens)

| Risk | Test that catches it |
|---|---|
| ROLLBACK stops at the wrong step (G8): the marker is set AFTER the push (one step short) or the loop ignores the marker (one step too far). | Step 7: BEGIN, move, ROLLBACK -> `location == before`, `marker_present` False, `undo_calls >= 1`, and an object created BEFORE the BEGIN still exists; step 8: `batch(undo=AUTO)` failing at the 3rd command -> `rolled_back: true`, the 1st and 2nd commands' effects gone, the pre-batch object still present. |
| A `#uid` ref resolves to a removed or renamed ID (R16 scan). | Step 4 (rename then `rna_get("#uid")` still resolves) and step 11 (`data_remove` then `rna_get("#uid")` -> not-found with `tried`). |
| `rna_set` bypasses the read-only / enum / range rules of `_rna_set_validated` (G3). | Step 3: `/type` set refused, `render.engine = "NOPE"` refused with `valid`, unknown prop refused with `closest`; with `stop_on_error=False` the valid items still apply (`applied == 2`). |
| Reply blow-up (request doc risk 1). | Step 9: `rna_get("SCENE:Scene", fields="all", depth=2, max_chars=2000)` -> `len(json) <= 2400` and `truncated` non-empty. Mutation: remove the cap -> step 9 FAIL. |
| Batch of legacy keys skips the integration gate (G9). | Margaret adds a `batch` containing `get_polyhaven_categories` with the toggle off: the item status is `error` with the gate text, the batch continues when `stop_on_error=False`. |
| Angles double-converted (G12): degrees in, degrees scaled again on read. | Step 3: set `rotation_euler=[90,0,0]` with `angles="DEGREES"`, read back default -> `1.5708`; read back with `angles="DEGREES"` -> `90`. |
| `exec_context="INVOKE_DEFAULT"` headless hangs a modal operator. | Step 5b: `call_operator("transform.translate", exec_context="INVOKE_DEFAULT")` with `bpy.app.background` -> refused BEFORE the call (`error` mentions `EXEC_DEFAULT`), elapsed < 50 ms. |
| `context.extra` key typo silently ignored (R19: `temp_override` accepts anything). | Step 5c: `extra={"edit_objekt": "#uid"}` -> `{error, valid: [16 keys]}` and the operator NOT called (object unchanged). |
| The batch wall cap is dead code, or a wall stop is reported as success without rollback (F12). | Step 8b: `addon._DRV_BATCH_WALL_S = 0.0` then a 3-command batch with `undo="AUTO"` -> the 1st command runs (R27), `stopped_at == 1`, items 2-3 `skipped`, `success` False, `error` contains "wall cap", `rolled_back` True and the 1st command's effect undone (R39); with `undo="NONE"` the same minus the rollback; restore the constant in `finally`. |
| Harness invariant broken: a driver step leaves EDIT mode or a temp scene behind. | `step()` asserts mode OBJECT and scene list unchanged after every step; `context.keep=true` test must end with an explicit mode reset in the step. |
| Two version sources bumped, one forgotten. | `tests/test_version_sync.py` with the 2.3.0 default (G13) fails on the stale file. |
| Server refuses a batch of exactly 200 or accepts 201 (off-by-one). | `test_drv_server.py`: 200 -> one `send_command`; 201 -> zero calls and the refusal text. |
| Non-ASCII in a step detail kills the cp1252 console. | Every `P()` goes through `A()` (headless_handlers.py:49); Margaret greps `drv_steps.py` for non-ASCII before T1 lands. |

### 12.7 Pre-verification oracle (QA and the coder start from the same requests)

`docs/apichecks/drv_oracle.md` (Barbara, next) lists the exact handler calls and the
expected reply keys for request-doc steps 1-9 and 11-13 in Python-literal form, e.g.
`call("drv_set", sets=[["#%d/location" % uid, [1, 2, 3]], ["#%d/rotation_euler" % uid, [90, 0, 0]]], angles="DEGREES")`
-> `applied == 2`, then `call("drv_get", refs=["#%d" % uid], fields="location,rotation_euler")`
-> `results[0].value.location == [1, 2, 3]` and `abs(results[0].value.rotation_euler[0] - 1.5708) < 1e-3`.
Margaret turns it into `drv_steps.py`; Linus runs it before posting a landing.

### 12.9 Debts (final list for the completion post, D-G4)

1. **Events (`events_subscribe` / `events_poll` / `events_unsubscribe`, handlers `drv_sub` / `drv_poll` /
   `drv_unsub`)**: first item of 2.4.0 (R38). Facts to build on: depsgraph_update_post fires headless after
   `view_layer.update()` with `is_updated_transform`; frame_change_post fires on `frame_set`; msgbus delivers
   NOTHING while a `--background` script runs, even after `publish_rna` (R18), so the `property` kind is
   live-only; timers never fire during a sleeping script, so `events_poll(wait_ms)` sleeps on the SERVER
   side; `@persistent` handlers must be registered in `register()` and removed in `unregister()`.
2. **msgbus live-only**: `rna_set(publish=True)` publishes but no harness step can assert delivery (R18); the
   D-G5 live batch is the only proof.
3. **`get_settings` / `set_settings` / `describe_settings` re-implemented on the driver** (request doc section 3):
   DEFERRED (Lead delta 6); R15 (`tests\settings_baseline.py -- --check`) pins their replies byte-identical.
4. **Recipe status notes** in the other seven request docs (which of their tools become driver recipes):
   written by the Planner of the phase that builds each doc.
5. **Tier 3** (schema dump, generated typed client, code-to-driver translator, push events over a second
   socket): not planned.
6. **B2 engine readiness** renumbers to 2.4.0 (section 11 E1 amended by this phase's ratification).
7. **Exit code 127 one-off**: one 4.3.2 harness run (addon 3d865a32, pre-landing) exited 127 after the SUMMARY
   line with no crash file; it did not reproduce at 20538431 or later. Unexplained; attributed by Linus to
   dereferencing `uv_sculpt` pointers before the R33 guard. Watch for it in future 4.3.2 runs.
8. **Embedded IDs** (node trees owned by a material, shape keys, etc.) are not in a `bpy.data` collection,
   so `#uid` cannot find them; they are reachable by ID + path only (`MATERIAL:Red/node_tree.nodes[...]`).
9. **NOT VERIFIED headless (Lead's list, carry to the completion post)**: GUI/live paths (area/region
   override, INVOKE, the modal warning on a real modal operator, msgbus delivery); a user undo between BEGIN
   and ROLLBACK; a transaction longer than `prefs.edit.undo_steps`; ROLLBACK when `ed.undo.poll()` is False
   (undo is available on both installs headless); the real 60 s wall (only `_DRV_BATCH_WALL_S = 0.0`
   measured); the exit-127 one-off (item 7); scan cost above 2000 IDs; `rna_set` on `prefs/*` paths.
   F12 (wall-capped AUTO batch neither rolled back nor errored) was caught by QA's cross-read of the frozen
   add-on and closed by R39 in L3.1.
10. **`add_modifier` out-of-range values** are now refused by the validator (`unset` with "x outside [min,
    max]") where the legacy setattr silently clamped: declared deviation (Linus L3 (2)), user-visible, in the
    release notes.
11. The two uncommitted doc lines from 2026-09-12 (`docs/README.md` row 18, `docs/apichecks/README.md`
