### 12.11 After-action reports (one paragraph + one rule each; collected as they land)

**Planner (Barbara)**: The contract landed in 10 minutes and was ratified with deltas rather than rewritten, because it
reused the repo's existing helpers (settings RNA readers, the exec-loaded step files, the FakeConn fixture) instead of
inventing seams, so every Lead delta was a fact-correction (no uid cache, Scene marker, static id_type table, unsafe
structs), not a redesign. The cost centre was the unrun check script: three rulings (R16/R17/R20) reversed D1.0 guesses
that a one-minute drvcheck run before writing would have settled. Writing the oracle from the contract gave QA and the
coder one source of truth and surfaced the only real contract gap (R30) before code existed. RULE: run every check
script that exists but has never run BEFORE the planner writes the first contract line; a measured fact table at t0 is
cheaper than three amendments at t+20.

**Add-on (Linus, addon.py 0eb7ec4c -> f2c680bb)**: Recon first paid for itself: running drvcheck.py on both installs before
the contract froze turned four of the doc's expectations around (undo works headless, msgbus does not deliver headless,
the context has a window, id_type struct names are not stable across versions) and every one became a ruling instead
of a bug found mid-build. The build went in three landings, each proven by the shared harness at a posted hash and
re-run from disk by QA; the expensive defects were all of one kind: a generic encoder that walks every property of
every struct meets platform edges a hand-written handler never did (the 4.3.2 uninitialised Paint struct that faults the
process, embedded IDs with no id_type, generator enums with a one-item fallback, a per-session cache mutated by
truncation). Each was found by a probe or smoke beside the harness, bisected to one member, and closed with a guard
plus a measured comment rather than a wider deny-list. The mutation evidence was honest about its limits: the planned
read-only mutant cannot discriminate because Blender refuses the assignment itself, so the discriminating mutants are
the ones on logic only the driver owns. Heredocs broke twice on quoting; every patch after that was a file written by
the editor and run from disk. RULE: before a generic RNA walker (encode, snapshot, find) ships, run it at full depth over
Scene, tool_settings, prefs and the window manager on every supported Blender build with a per-property print flushed
before each getattr, and treat a process crash as a contract fact recorded as a struct-family deny-set with the
crash-file frame, because an access violation is not an exception and no try/except will save the session.

**Server + docs (Grace)**: The server slice landed in three thin layers (7 + 4 + 2 wrappers) against a contract she had
not seen the add-on side of, with a FakeConn self-check that asserted the exact wire tuple of every wrapper before each
landing; that is what let QA's test_drv_server.py pass 24/24 on first contact apart from one real defect class (a JSON
string value echoed as the reply) that the chokepoint ruling R31 closed for all 11 callers in one line. What cost time:
the contract moved three times (D1.0 -> D1.3) and the rulings outran the file on disk (R22-R28, then R30-R37 were
DM-only for a while), so she built from DMs and declared every gap; a seam test (byte-identity vs 2.2) contradicted the
contract (G11b) and needed a ruling; two docs assertions by the Lead came from a truncated grep view, not from the file.
Recon paid for itself twice: the exact-equality tools-list hazard became R11/R29 before it bit, and the dump_tools_list
caveat (PowerShell `>` writes UTF-16) stopped a bad baseline. Docs went in one pass because every reply shape was already
written down in 12.2.2 and the add-on was on disk to read from. RULE: freeze the rulings FILE before the first landing and
make every DM ruling a one-line append to it in the same minute, because a coder who builds from a DM and a tester who
builds from the file will disagree on exact error text, and that disagreement surfaces as red tests on a correct landing.

**QA (Margaret)**: The test plan was written from the request doc before any code existed, so the nine contract
inconsistencies (Tier 2 leaking into Tier 1 steps, unspecified wire shapes, the cap's home, the 169+16 count, untestable
caps) became rulings before a coder built against them; the settings-reply fixture closed a gate that had no fixture at
all; every finding went out as repro + expected/actual + a red test the owner could run from disk, which is why F4 (a
defect class across 11 call sites), F9 (a process crash with the stack frame) and F12 (a contract clause the code could
not reach) each closed in one landing. Running the harness against the moving tree between landings, labelled
pre-landing, found F6-F10 hours before the acceptance pairs would have. What cost time: her own fixture bugs (an empty
mesh for bevel, a uid read after the remove, a read-only step that changed the active object, a material already purged,
a bad-ref expectation on drv_get) each cost a 10-minute pair. One fact for the successor: the harness exec-loads
drv_steps.py when it reaches the driver section, so editing the file while a run is in flight changes that run. RULE:
before you post a finding against a coder, run a 20-second probe that reproduces it with the add-on loaded the way the
harness loads it, so the DM carries the exact reply and the test carries the exact assertion, and the same probe tells
you in one run whether the next FAIL is theirs or yours.

### 13.0 House rules (PLAN 12.0 verbatim + R-B2-1..19 by reference)

ACK every assignment, finding, ruling and gate signal by DM within one poll; no ACK in 5 min =
re-assigned; one file = one owner; contract changes only via the Planner as a versioned
amendment (v1.1, v1.2, ...) ratified by the Lead; every deviation declared even when trivial;
peer agreements need Lead ratification.

Standing rulings (not reopened): nobody commits, deploys, launches a Blender GUI or the live MCP server; live
copies (`B:\...`, `D:\blender-4.3.2-windows-x64\MCP\`, `%APPDATA%` add-ons) are never edited; headless command
is `<install>\blender.exe --background --factory-startup --python <script>`; evidence = pasted output, PASS/FAIL
per step, `git hash-object <file>` for every landing, in the same minute as the landing; 4.x/5.x differences by
try-assign or hasattr, NEVER `bpy.app.version` compares (M7 grep stays 0); assertions are never weakened;
everything scripted prints ASCII only. Progress, hashes and test output go on the owner's kanban card (board
`team-lohjud`, db `creative-suite`), the room carries questions, rulings and one-line signals.

R-B2-1..19 and D1-D9 are the file `docs/LEAD-RULINGS-B2.md` (owner Lead; later ruling wins). Toolchain (R-B2-2): harness
`<install>\blender.exe --background --factory-startup --python tests\headless_handlers.py -- --b0 --b1 --drv --b2`
on 4.3.2 and 5.2.1; venv `.venv\Scripts\python -m pytest tests -q`, no env vars. Owners (R-B2-4): `addon.py` Linus;
`src/blender_mcp/server.py`, `__init__.py`, `pyproject.toml`, `TOOLS.md`, `README.md`, `docs/release-notes/v2.4.0.md`
Grace; `tests/**` Margaret; `docs/PLAN.md` + `docs/apichecks/*` Barbara. Identities `@x4rah`.
Note for every team-tool payload: no double-quote characters (start payloads truncate at the first one).

The additive-param whitelist is enumerated in 13.4.1 and copied, never guessed. | `test_f6_connection.py:223` is exact identity vs 2.3 and goes red on the first new tool. |

FINAL for 2.4.0 unless the conditional L4 (Tier 2, +7 tools) rides. | R-B2-13 (three landings); 11.2 count; user directive 18:19. |

The R-B2-9 seam (`_drv_batch` / `_drv_op`) stays available and UNUSED; driver doc s5's engine-readiness row gets a note at B2-G4. | D5: every one needs bmesh, the evaluated depsgraph, version-guarded operator kwargs or a readback loop; one idiom for 22 tools beats two. |
| H5 | Server wrappers: parse-before-connect, enum refusals with zero sends (Margaret S2), every accepted param sent on the wire (None included, S1 pins the tuple), `Error: <text>` rendering; no wrapper renders its own reply from driver calls. | R12/R31 idiom; H4. |
| H6 | `_ENGINE_PRESETS` (exporter kwargs per engine) AND `_PROFILE_NAMES` (collision/socket/LOD naming per profile) both live in the ADD-ON; `get_export_preset` is handler `get_export_preset` (one round trip, returns the table entry). | R-B2-14: one table, one owner of the evaluated counts. Supersedes 11.2 and TEST-PLAN-B2 S3/S4/E1. |
| H7 | `<file>.manifest.json` is written by the ADD-ON beside the file (it owns the counts); `batch_manifest.json` is written SERVER-side by the `batch_export` loop. No manifest on an error reply. | R-B2-14. |
| H8 | Object selection: `objects` = comma list with fnmatch globs (add-on `_names_arg` 5143 + `_glob_names`), or `collection=`; both/neither -> error; unknown names -> `{error, unknown: [...]}` and NOTHING applied. Single-mesh tools take `mesh` (13.9 Q12 list). R-B2-41 / B2.18 (Margaret's finding): a LITERAL name is always addressable: each entry tries an EXACT name match first and falls back to fnmatch only when nothing matches exactly (add-on, Linus's RA landing; the server's `batch_export` expansion gets the same exact-first rule as RS-15, Grace at her docs resume); no wire change, 2.2 callers unaffected (an exact hit was always also a glob hit). A name containing `,` stays a documented limitation (TOOLS.md: use `collection=` or the driver's `#uid`); as built (B2.20, Linus's extension of R-B2-41, no wire change): a WHOLE `objects=` string that equals one object name is kept unsplit, so `objects="Wall, left"` works alone while `"A,Wall, left"` still splits. Tests: RA4 asserts `Panel[1]`, `Star*Prop`, `Q?Mark` addressable by `objects=` on the add-on; S-case `batch_export(objects="Panel[1]")` at the RS-15 hash. | House idiom; validate before mutate. |
| H9 | Profiles `GENERIC / UNITY / UNREAL / GODOT / BEVY / TIMBERMESH`; engines = profiles + `GENERIC_GLTF / GENERIC_FBX`. Enum refusals happen on the SERVER with zero sends (`Error: <param> must be one of [...]`, Margaret S2) AND again in the add-on (`{error, valid}`) for direct callers (batch). | R12/R31 idiom; the harness calls handlers directly. |
| H10 | `validate_asset` check ids = fixed set of 22 (13.2.3); `fix=False` is READ-ONLY (bmesh copies, no ops, no selection change; harness `readonly=True`); `fix=True` applies the 6 safe fixes in a fixed order and re-runs the checks. | Margaret Q13/V1; a fixed id set lets tests key on names. |
| H11 | Landings (R-B2-13): L1 = version bump + H12 + H13 + 13.2.1 fixes + 13.2.2 validation (first end-to-end proof = `export_object` new params + `get_mesh_stats` evaluated triangles, then `validate_asset` reading the same helpers); L2 = 13.2.3 + 13.2.4; L3 = 13.2.5 + 13.2.6. T-landings precede coder landings. | Validation shares `_eval_tri_count` / `_mesh_issue_indices` with the `get_mesh_stats` fix; fewer gates. |
| H12 | Debt #13 (R-B2-11, R-B2-18, D2 = GO in L1, no gate): Linus L1 removes the four legacy Scene `StringProperty` registrations (addon.py ~9958); `_migrate_legacy_secrets` (152-173) reads `scene.get(key)` first, then `scene.bl_system_properties_get(do_create=False)` when the method exists, moves the value to prefs and deletes the key from EVERY group holding it (B2.8, Linus's measured refinement: 5.2.1 opening a 4.x file carries the value in BOTH groups); the free-trial operator (9923) writes prefs. Margaret owns TWO fixtures: `tests/fixtures/legacy_secrets_1.6.0.blend` (4.3.2-written, opened on both installs) and `tests/fixtures/legacy_secrets_5x.blend` (5.2.1-written, 5.2.1 only), generated from Linus's DM snippet; harness step G7 opens each and asserts prefs hold the values and the keys are gone. | Linus (18:08): after unregistration `scene.get()` returns the value on both installs for a 4.3.2 save, `getattr` raises; a 5.2.1 save stores it in the SYSTEM idprop group where `scene.get()` is None. |

### 13.2 Interface contract (B2.2)

Conventions. Server params are FastMCP-typed (`str` for lists/JSON, `bool`, `int`, `float`); REQUIRED params have
no default in the signature; everything else is optional with the default shown. Wire: the server sends EVERY
param it accepts (None included) so the add-on default never diverges from the docstring (Margaret S1 pins the
tuple). JSON params: dict -> `_parse_json_object` (server.py 3432), list-or-dict -> `_parse_json_any` (5770,
scalars refused R31), list of strings -> `_parse_refs` (3449). Add-on errors are dicts `{error: str, ...}` with
`valid` / `closest` / `unknown` where stated; wrappers render `Error: <text>` (+ ` valid: [...]` when present) and
never raise. Degrees everywhere (H14). Counts named `triangles` are EVALUATED
(`evaluated_get(depsgraph)` + `calc_loop_triangles`, E13) unless suffixed `_simple`.

#### 13.2.0 Side per tool (H4 = Lead's H23: all add-on; handler key == tool name)

| Tool(s) | Handler key(s) | Note |
|---|---|---|
| `export_object`, `import_file`, `get_mesh_stats`, `get_scene_info`, `apply_modifier`, `set_origin` | existing keys | section 1 fixes, additive params (13.4.1 whitelist) |
| `validate_asset`, `find_mesh_issues`, `compare_meshes`, `apply_transforms`, `fix_normals`, `cleanup_mesh` | same names | L1 |
| `decimate_mesh`, `generate_lods`, `set_normals`, `triangulate_for_export`, `get_triangle_budget`, `create_collision`, `get_collision_info`, `check_collision_fit` | same names | L2 |
| `add_socket`, `list_sockets`, `set_pivot`, `snap_to_grid`, `build_export_hierarchy`, `export_for_engine`, `get_export_preset` | same names | L3 |
| `batch_export` | none | server loop over `export_for_engine` sends; writes `batch_manifest.json` (H7) |

Driver doc s5 said several of these become recipes; D5 overrules for this phase (one idiom for 22 tools). The
`_drv_batch` / `_drv_op` seam (R-B2-9) is not built unless a later phase needs it.

#### 13.2.1 Fixes to existing tools (L1; additive params only, exact whitelist in 13.4.1)

| Tool | Added params (server type = default) | Reply additions | Error shape |
|---|---|---|---|
| `export_object` | `objects: str = None` (comma list, alternative to `name`; both given -> error), `apply_modifiers: bool = True`, `axis_forward: str = None`, `axis_up: str = None`, `global_scale: float = None`, `use_tspace: bool = None`, `use_triangles: bool = None`, `use_custom_props: bool = None`, `colors_type: str = None`, `export_apply: bool = None`, `export_tangents: bool = None`, `export_extras: bool = None`, `export_yup: bool = None`, `export_attributes: bool = None`, `export_vertex_color: str = None`, `export_animation_mode: str = None`. None = exporter default; enum values try-assigned, error quotes the live tuple (D9). | `size_bytes: int`, `object_count: int`, `vertices_written: int`, `triangles_written: int`, `warnings: [str]` (texts: `unapplied scale on <obj>`, `no UV layer on <obj>`, `ngons on <obj> with use_triangles off`, `material <m> has no nodes`), `transform_baked: bool` (present for every format, True only for OBJ on 4.x, E10), B2.9: `modifiers_applied: bool` (counts are evaluated only when modifiers were applied), `export_options` for obj/stl/ply beside `fbx_options`/`gltf_options`, glTF `export_apply` defaults to `apply_modifiers` | `{error}`; unknown names `{error, unknown}` |
| `import_file` | `axis_forward: str = None`, `axis_up: str = None`, `global_scale: float = None`, `use_custom_normals: bool = True`, `use_image_search: bool = None`, `ignore_leaf_bones: bool = None`, `import_shading: str = None` (glTF), `merge_vertices: bool = None` (glTF); None = importer default; kwargs not in the live operator are reported under `ignored` (try/except TypeError, E8/E9) | `objects: [{name, type, vertices, triangles, has_custom_normals, uv_layers: [str], materials: [str]}]`; existing keys `imported_objects`, `armatures`, `actions`, `new_actions`, `bone_shape_objects` kept (R-B2-17 (8)) | `{error}` |

| `get_scene_info` | server `limit: int = None` (None = not forwarded, so the 2.2 wire tuple is unchanged; the add-on default is 10; 0 = all; R-B2-32 / B2.12, review RS-1: the object listing was capped at the first 10 objects, addon.py:778; `batch_export` sends `limit=0`) | `total_triangles`, `total_vertices` (MESH objects, evaluated), per object entry `triangles`, `hidden: bool` (= `not obj.visible_get()`, which also covers collection-level hiding; R-B2-17 (7), B2.10 text), `parent: <name>|null` (R-B2-28 / B2.7; entries carried no parent key at addon f2c680bb) and `collections: [str]` (`users_collection` names; R-B2-30 / B2.11, Linus L1.1; absent at ee6b927f); `settings_summary.units` already present (no change) | unchanged; `settings_baseline` fixture unaffected (it covers settings replies, not get_scene_info; S9 re-cut only on a ruling) |

#### 13.2.2 Validation (L1 add-on; wrappers Grace L1; steps V1-V12)

`validate_asset` (handler `validate_asset`)
- Params: `objects: str = None`, `collection: str = None` (exactly one), `profile: str = "GENERIC"`, `max_triangles: int = None`, `require_uv: bool = True`, `require_uv2: bool = False`, `max_bone_influences: int = 4`, `fix: bool = False`.
- Reply: `{profile, verdict: PASS|WARN|FAIL, summary: {pass: n, warn: n, fail: n} (counts OBJECTS by their verdict, R-B2-17 (6)), objects: {name: {verdict, type, checks: {<id>: {status, detail: str, count: int, sample_indices: [<=20 int]}}}}, fixes_available: [{object, check, fix}], fixes_applied: [{object, check, fix, detail}]}`; `fixes_available` and `fixes_applied` are ALWAYS present (empty lists when none). Non-MESH objects: `checks: {}`, `verdict: PASS`, `detail: not a mesh` at object level.
- Check ids (22, every one present per mesh): `unapplied_transform` FAIL; `negative_scale` FAIL; `origin_bottom_center` WARN; `dimensions` WARN (max extent x `scale_length` > 100 m or < 1 cm, unit-aware, B2.9; a zero-thickness plane never WARNs); `ngons` FAIL when the profile exports tangents (UNITY, UNREAL, GODOT, BEVY; E5) else WARN; `non_manifold` WARN (R-B2-29: edges with more than 2 faces, wire edges, opposed-winding edges; boundary edges EXCLUDED and reported in `detail` as `boundary: <n>`); `loose` WARN; `degenerate` FAIL (zero-area faces); `doubles` WARN (1e-5); `inconsistent_normals` FAIL (faces flipped by a `recalc_face_normals` dry-run on a bmesh COPY, count = flipped); `missing_uv` FAIL when `require_uv`; `uv_out_of_bounds` WARN; `missing_uv2` FAIL when `require_uv2`; `vertex_count_16bit` WARN (`len(mesh.vertices)` > 65535, pre-split; exporters may split more, Q4); `triangle_budget` FAIL over `max_triangles` (PASS when None); `material_slots` WARN (empty slot or slot unused by any face); `materials_without_nodes` WARN; `images_missing` FAIL (image datablock whose `filepath_from_user()` does not exist and is not packed); `bone_influences` WARN (vertices with more than `max_bone_influences` groups of weight > 0, Q5); `illegal_names` WARN (object or material name with space, `.`, or non-ASCII for UNITY/UNREAL/GODOT/BEVY; TIMBERMESH: none); `name_collisions` WARN (names equal after stripping `.NNN`); `hierarchy` (R-B2-17 (5)): no children = PASS; a collision-typed child = a MESH child with `display_type == WIRE` and `hide_render` OR a name matching ANY profile's collision rule; FAIL when such a child's name does not follow the asked profile's rule (UNREAL `UCX_<mesh>_NN`, GODOT `-col`/`-convcol`; UNITY/GENERIC/BEVY accept any), and for TIMBERMESH when no `#` root is in the chain; else PASS.
- Safe fixes (`fix=True`, order): `apply_transforms` (rotation + scale; after a negative-scale apply `fix_normals(OUTSIDE)` runs and is recorded under `negative_scale`, B2.9) -> `fix_normals(OUTSIDE)` -> `remove_doubles 1e-5` -> `delete_loose` -> `triangulate ngons` (bmesh triangulate on faces with > 4 verts only) -> `rename illegal chars to _`. Post-fix the checks re-run and the reply carries the post-fix verdict. Every 13.2.2 reply carries `success: true` (house idiom, B2.9); `material_slots.detail` lists `index=material`; `name_collisions` flags every twin across `bpy.data.objects`.
- Errors: `{error, valid}` bad profile; `{error, unknown}`.

| Tool = handler | Params | Reply | Errors |
|---|---|---|---|
| `find_mesh_issues` | `mesh: str` (REQUIRED), `issue: str` (REQUIRED: `NON_MANIFOLD / NGONS / LOOSE / DEGENERATE / DOUBLES / FLIPPED / INTERIOR_FACES / BOUNDARY`), `render: bool = False`, `angle: str = "iso_front_right"`, `max_indices: int = 500` | `{mesh, issue, element: VERT|EDGE|FACE, count, indices: [int], truncated: bool}`; `render=True` adds `image_path` (server returns the PNG via `_safe_image_return`, copy `find_unweighted_vertices(render=True)`); counts via edit-mode selects + `bmesh.from_edit_mesh` (E7), OBJECT mode restored in `finally` | `{error, valid}`; headless `render=True` -> `{error: ... needs a GUI session, background: true}` (R20) |
| `compare_meshes` | `mesh_a: str`, `mesh_b: str` (REQUIRED), `tolerance: float = 1e-5`, `compare: str = "ALL"` (comma list of `VERTS / ORDER / NORMALS / UVS / TANGENTS / MATERIALS / GROUPS`) | `{mesh_a, mesh_b, same: bool, unit_test_compare: str, vertices: {a, b, delta}, triangles: {a, b, delta}, positions: {differing: int, max_delta: float}|null (null when counts differ), order_match: bool|null, custom_normals: {a: bool, b: bool, max_angle_deg: float|null}, uvs: {layers_a: [str], layers_b: [str], max_delta: float|null}, tangents: {a: bool, b: bool}, materials: {a: [str], b: [str], same: bool}, vertex_groups: {a: [str], b: [str], same: bool}}`; `same` = `unit_test_compare == "Same"` (E13 wording differs per version); numpy via `foreach_get` into float64 (M12). R-B2-27 (B2.6): sections NOT requested in `compare` are OMITTED from the reply; only `mesh_a, mesh_b, same, unit_test_compare, vertices, triangles` are always present; `null` is reserved for requested-but-not-computable (`positions` when counts differ, `max_angle_deg` when neither mesh has custom normals). `order_match` (B2.9) = for every i, B[i] is as close to A[i] as B's nearest vertex to A[i] (KD-tree; duplicate-vertex ties allowed) | `{error, valid}` bad compare token |
| `fix_normals` | `objects: str` (REQUIRED), `mode: str = "OUTSIDE"` (`OUTSIDE / INSIDE / FLIP / CLEAR_CUSTOM`), `also_sharp_by_angle: float = None` | `{objects: [str], mode, faces_flipped: {name: int}, custom_normals_cleared: [str], sharp_edges_marked: {name: int}}` (last key only when the angle is given); CLEAR_CUSTOM = `customdata_custom_splitnormals_clear()` (E4) | `{error, valid}` |
| `cleanup_mesh` | `objects: str` (REQUIRED), `remove_doubles: float = 0.0001` (0 = skip), `delete_loose: bool = True`, `dissolve_degenerate: bool = True`, `fill_holes: int = 0`, `limited_dissolve: float = None` (deg) | `{objects, per_object: {name: {doubles_removed, loose_verts_removed, loose_edges_removed, degenerate_dissolved, faces_dissolved, holes_filled, faces_before, faces_after}}}` one `_bmesh_edit` pass (addon.py 1123); order (B2.9): dissolve degenerate BEFORE remove_doubles (a zero-area face's coincident verts are not doubles) | `{error}` |

| `apply_transforms` | `objects: str` (REQUIRED), `location: bool = False`, `rotation: bool = True`, `scale: bool = True`, `keep_children_world: bool = True` | `{objects: [str], applied: {location, rotation, scale}, isolated: [names whose data.users went > 1 -> 1 via isolate_users=True], negative_scale: [names with a scale component < 0 before; applied anyway], flip_corrected: [names] (5.2 `corrective_flip_normals=True` via try/except TypeError, E6; [] on 4.3.2)}`; `transform_apply` under `_selection_scope` with the objects selected and the first active; `keep_children_world=True` is IMPLEMENTED (R-B2-39 / B2.15: each child's `matrix_world` is restored after the apply), False leaves children where the operator puts them; V10 asserts a child's world vertex coords unchanged | `{error, unknown}` |

Decisive fact: comparing against a default string misclassifies an explicit `0.5,0.25,0.1` + targets as the targets form, a silent wrong answer), `naming: str = "{name}_LOD{level}"`, `collection: str = None`, `parent_to_lod0: bool = False`, `mode: str = "COLLAPSE"`, `symmetry_axis: str = None`, `keep_uv_seams: bool = True`, `preserve_silhouette_group: str = None`, `transfer_normals: bool = False` | `{source: <base name>, lod0: <name>, levels: [{level, name, triangles, ratio}], collection: str|null, custom_normals_transferred: bool}`; an existing `_LOD<n>` suffix on the source is STRIPPED to get `base` (R-B2-17 (9)); every target name is checked BEFORE any change and a collision returns `{error, existing: [names]}` with the scene untouched; materials shared; each level is a mesh COPY; a failure after the rename restores the name | `{error}` bad ratios; `{error, existing}` collisions (no partial scene change) |

| Tool = handler | Params | Reply | Errors |
|---|---|---|---|

| `get_collision_info` | `mesh: str` (REQUIRED) | `{mesh, proxies: [{name, profile, triangles, is_child: bool}]}` by every profile's naming rule (`_PROFILE_NAMES`) | `{error}` |

#### 13.2.5 Sockets, pivots and hierarchy (L3 add-on; steps S1-S5)

| Tool = handler | Params | Reply | Errors |
|---|---|---|---|
| `add_socket` | `parent: str`, `name: str` (REQUIRED), `location: str = "0,0,0"`, `rotation: str = "0,0,0"` (deg), `space: str = "LOCAL"` (`LOCAL / WORLD`), `profile: str = "UNREAL"` (`UNREAL / TIMBERMESH / UNITY / GENERIC`), `bone: str = None`, `display_size: float = 0.1`, `snap_to: str = "NONE"` (`NONE / SURFACE / VERTEX`), `vertex_index: int = None` | `{name: <full>, parent, bone: str|null, profile, location_world: [3], rotation_world: [3] deg, matrix_world: [4x[4]]}`; full name UNREAL `SOCKET_<name>`, TIMBERMESH `#<name>`, else `<name>`; SURFACE = `closest_point_on_mesh` from the given location, VERTEX = `vertex_index` world position | `{error}` TIMBERMESH with no `#` root in the parent chain; `{error}` bone on a non-ARMATURE parent; `{error, valid}` |
| `list_sockets` | `parent: str = None` | `{parent, count, sockets: [{name, profile: UNREAL|TIMBERMESH|GENERIC, parent, bone: str|null, location_local: [3], location_world: [3], rotation_local: [3] deg}]}` over EMPTY objects whose name starts with `SOCKET_` or `#`, or (parent given) every EMPTY child of `parent`; read-only | `{error}` unknown parent |
| `set_pivot` | `objects: str` (REQUIRED), `mode: str = "BOTTOM_CENTER"` (`BOTTOM_CENTER / CENTER / TOP_CENTER / WORLD_ORIGIN / CURSOR / CUSTOM`), `location: str = None` (CUSTOM), `keep_world: bool = True` | `{objects, mode, keep_world, per_object: {name: {new_location: [3]}}}`; loops the extended `set_origin` in-process (map CENTER -> ORIGIN_GEOMETRY, WORLD_ORIGIN -> CUSTOM at 0,0,0, CURSOR -> ORIGIN_CURSOR); `keep_world=True`: world vertex coords unchanged (S3 step); `keep_world=False` (R-B2-39 / B2.15): the geometry MOVES, no location offset (S3 asserts world vertices moved by the delta) | `{error, valid}`; `{error}` CUSTOM without location |
| `snap_to_grid` | `objects: str` (REQUIRED), `grid: float = 1.0` (> 0), `axes: str = "XYZ"`, `what: str = "ORIGIN"` (`ORIGIN / BOUNDS_MIN / BOUNDS_CENTER`) | `{objects, grid, axes, what, per_object: {name: {before: [3], after: [3]}}}`; anchor = origin or the world bbox corner/centre, rounded on the chosen axes, delta applied to `location` | `{error}` grid <= 0; `{error, valid}` |
| `build_export_hierarchy` | `root_name: str`, `meshes: str` (REQUIRED), `collision: str = None`, `sockets: str = None`, `lods: str = None`, `profile: str = "UNREAL"` | `{root, profile, created_root: bool, tree: {name, children: [...]}, custom_props_set: {object: {key: value}}}`; reuses an EMPTY named `root_name` or creates one; meshes under root; LODs under root with `lod_group` int custom prop (UNREAL); collision under its mesh (UNREAL/GENERIC/UNITY) or as root siblings (GODOT); sockets under their mesh | `{error, unknown}` |

#### 13.2.6 Engine export (L3; steps E1-E6)

`get_export_preset` (add-on handler `get_export_preset`, table `_ENGINE_PRESETS` in the add-on, H6)
- Params: `engine: str` (REQUIRED; server refuses an unknown engine with zero sends, the add-on again with `{error, valid}`).
- Reply: `{engine, format: fbx|glb|timbermesh, exporter: fbx|gltf|timbermesh, kwargs: {...}, axis: {forward, up}|null, units: {scale_length, length_unit}, naming: {collision, socket, lod}, notes: [str]}`.
- Table (single source; `export_for_engine` applies the same entry): UNITY fbx `{axis_forward: -Z, axis_up: Y, apply_scale_options: FBX_SCALE_ALL, global_scale: 1.0, use_tspace: true, mesh_smooth_type: FACE, add_leaf_bones: false, bake_space_transform: false, use_custom_props: true}` units 1.0 METERS; UNREAL fbx `{axis_forward: -Y, axis_up: Z, global_scale: 1.0, apply_scale_options: FBX_SCALE_NONE, use_tspace: true, mesh_smooth_type: FACE, add_leaf_bones: false, use_custom_props: true}` units 0.01 CENTIMETERS, note `set_scene_units(UNREAL)`; GODOT and BEVY glb `{export_yup: true, export_apply: true, export_tangents: true, export_extras: true, export_attributes: true, export_vertex_color: MATERIAL, export_image_format: AUTO}` units 1.0 METERS; TIMBERMESH timbermesh `{merge_meshes: true, single_animation: true, use_vertex_animations: false}`; GENERIC_FBX fbx `{}`; GENERIC_GLTF glb `{}`. Naming per profile = `_PROFILE_NAMES` (13.2.4 collision names, 13.2.5 socket names, LOD `{name}_LOD{level}`).

`export_for_engine` (add-on handler `export_for_engine`)
- Params: `objects: str = None` XOR `collection: str = None`, `engine: str` (REQUIRED), `filepath: str = None` (server default `_default_output_path("<first root>.<ext>")` else None = add-on temp path `<tempdir>/blender_export_<pid>.<ext>`), `include_hierarchy: bool = True`, `include_collision: bool = True`, `include_sockets: bool = True`, `include_lods: bool = True`, `include_animation: bool = False`, `apply_modifiers: bool = True`, `triangulate: bool = True`, `validate: bool = True`, `textures: str = "COPY"` (`COPY / EMBED / NONE / KEEP_PATHS`), `overrides: str = None` (JSON object via `_parse_json_object`; exporter kwargs merged over the preset; `force: true` exports despite FAIL).
- Wire: every param as named (overrides as a dict or null).
- Reply: `{filepath, engine, format, exporter, size_bytes, manifest_path, objects: [str], triangles, materials: [str], textures_copied: [str], validation: {verdict, fail: n, warn: n}|null, warnings: [str], exporter_kwargs: {...as applied}, timbermesh_root: str|null}`; on validation FAIL without force: `{error: validation FAIL on <n> check(s): <ids>; pass overrides with force true to export anyway, validation: {...}}` and NO file, NO manifest. `triangulate=True` (D6, NON-destructive): a temporary Triangulate modifier (`keep_custom_normals=True`) is added to each exported mesh and removed in `finally`; FBX `use_mesh_modifiers=True` / glTF `export_apply=True` evaluate it, so the file is triangulated and tangents compute on the evaluated mesh (E5) while the scene mesh keeps its polygons (E2 asserts source polygon count unchanged, re-import triangles == manifest `triangles`). UNREAL + `scale_length != 0.01` -> warning `scene scale_length is <x>, Unreal expects 0.01: run set_scene_units(UNREAL)`; `mesh_smooth_type` validated against the live enum (E8); TIMBERMESH per H17 (temporary collection removed in `finally`; requires a `#` root).
- Manifest (add-on, H7): `<filepath>.manifest.json` = the reply minus `manifest_path` and `success` plus `{manifest_version: 1, written_at: <ISO>, blender: <version string>, preset: <engine>}`, `sort_keys`.
- Review fixes ruled at G3.5 for the add-on (docs/REVIEW-B2-addon.md dfd68a5f, RA-1..RA-17, card b2-review-addon; contract-relevant ones, B2.15): `triangulate=True` with `apply_modifiers=False` is REFUSED before any mutation, `{error: "triangulate=True requires apply_modifiers=True"}` (R-B2-38, RA-8); `textures=COPY` resolves basename collisions as `<parentfolder>_<basename>`, `textures_copied` lists the files actually written and the exporter points at them (RA-17); internal callers (the validate fix path, `export_for_engine`) pass object references, never the comma/glob parser, so names containing `, [ * ?` work (RA-4); `fix_normals` / `cleanup_mesh` validate the whole list before touching anything (RA-6, H8); `decimate_mesh(new_object)` and `create_collision(MESH_DECIMATED)` remove their orphans on error (RA-7); the rotation-identity test is keyed on `rotation_mode` (AXIS_ANGLE included, RA-5); `get_scene_info` evaluates each mesh once (RA-10). READ PATHS (R-B2-36 as clarified 19:34, B2.17, Linus's RA-2 deviation accepted): pure reads (`get_mesh_stats`, `validate_asset(fix=False)`) use `bmesh.new().from_mesh` with NO scope (nothing to restore); any read that must enter edit mode (`find_mesh_issues` select-op issues such as NON_MANIFOLD / INTERIOR_FACES) runs inside `_selection_scope` + `_mode_restore` so the user's prior mode and active object come back, net unchanged. Margaret's RA2 step asserts all three with another object in EDIT mode. As built (Linus RA landing ee9b9dc0, B2.20): pure reads = `bmesh.new().from_mesh` with no scope for `get_mesh_stats`, `validate_asset`, `compare_meshes` and `find_mesh_issues` BOUNDARY / LOOSE / DEGENERATE / DOUBLES / FLIPPED / NON_MANIFOLD (`BMEdge.is_manifold` False) / NGONS; INTERIOR_FACES keeps the select op (its region rule is not a per-face predicate) inside `_selection_scope` + `_mesh_select_scope` + `_mode_restore`. EDIT-mode read fix (E20): every read handler first flushes an EDIT-mode mesh with `update_from_editmode()` (no mode change) and `validate_asset` reads `uv_out_of_bounds` from the BMesh copy, because under RNA `Mesh.uv_layers[0].data` has length 0 while an edit-mesh exists (foreach_get length mismatch) and the evaluated mesh of a co-edited object has 0 polygons in --background (so `get_mesh_stats.triangles` is 0 for an object in EDIT mode: Blender, not the add-on); SCULPT mode is not flushed (not verified). Driver (2.3.0 code in the same tree, section 12): `transaction ROLLBACK` with the marker already gone returns `{error: "transaction marker not found; nothing reverted", success: false, undo_calls: 0}` (RA-9); `drv_call` resolves POINTER args through `_drv_pointer_value` like `drv_new`/`drv_set` (RA-12).
- As built in Linus L3 (addon e9c78fe0; declared deviations a-l folded as B2.13 text): (a) validation runs on the RENDERABLE meshes only; collision-typed meshes (WIRE + hide_render, or a profile collision name) are excluded from `validate_asset` (no UVs/materials by design) but still exported and counted; (b) exporter kwargs absent on the live operator are DROPPED with a `warnings` entry (e.g. `export_vertex_color_name` on 4.3.2), enum kwargs are checked against the live enum -> `{error, valid}`; (c) FBX `object_types` defaults to EMPTY/MESH/ARMATURE/OTHER; `textures`: EMBED = FBX `path_mode COPY` + `embed_textures`, NONE = FBX `path_mode STRIP` / glTF `export_image_format NONE`, COPY = files copied beside the export (`textures_copied` absolute paths), KEEP_PATHS = exporter default; (d) `include_*` filters: collision = WIRE + hide_render mesh or a profile collision name, socket = EMPTY child named `SOCKET_`/`#` or any EMPTY child of a mesh, lod = `_LOD<n>` with n >= 1; the requested roots are never filtered; (e) Timbermesh root = the first `#` object in the set whose parent is outside the set; temp collection `MCP_TmpTimbermesh_<pid>` removed in `finally`; (f) `add_socket`: duplicate names get Blender's `.001` suffix (E12), WORLD rotation applied as given, LOCAL relative to the parent, `snap_to` VERTEX/SURFACE override `space`; (g) `list_sockets` as specified; (h) `set_pivot` echoes `keep_world`, refuses non-mesh objects for the bbox modes; (i) `snap_to_grid` moves `matrix_world.translation` (parent-safe), `axes` any subset of XYZ; (j) `build_export_hierarchy` sets `lod_group` on meshes too (0, or the `_LOD<n>` index), collision/socket targets by name prefix else the first mesh, tree nodes carry `type`; (k) `get_scene_info.limit` refuses non-integers; (l) the manifest shape above. `_hull_bmesh` is rebuilt from the vertex cloud (remove_doubles, hull, interior/unused deleted, degenerate dissolved, triangulated, normals recalculated) so every CONVEX / SLICED_CONVEX proxy is a closed manifold hull (R-B2-34).
- Server text: `Exported <engine> -> <filepath> (<size> bytes, N objects, T tris); manifest: <path>; validation: <verdict>; warnings: ...`.

`batch_export` (SERVER loop; no handler)
- Params: `objects: str = None`, `collection: str = None`, `engine: str` (REQUIRED), `output_dir: str = None` (default `_settings.get("output_dir")` else `<tempdir>/blender_batch_export`), `naming: str = "{name}"`, `one_file_per: str = "OBJECT"` (`OBJECT / ROOT / COLLECTION`), `skip_hidden: bool = True`, `continue_on_error: bool = True`.
- Roots: ONE `get_scene_info` send with `limit=0` (R-B2-28 / B2.7, R-B2-32), always made: entries with `type == MESH` and `parent == null`, minus `hidden` ones when `skip_hidden`; `collection` filter = the entry's `collections` list contains the name (R-B2-30); when no entry carries `collections` (an older add-on), `batch_export(collection=...)` refuses after the scene call and before any export send with `Error: collection filter unavailable: get_scene_info entries carry no collection membership` (Grace L3.1 as built). `drv_find` leaves the server loop entirely. Then one `export_for_engine` send per root (`filepath = <output_dir>/<naming>.<ext>`); an error reply = row FAIL, continue iff `continue_on_error`.
- `one_file_per` (R-B2-33 / B2.12, review RS-8): OBJECT sends `include_hierarchy=False` (one file per object, no children); ROOT sends `include_hierarchy=True` (each root with its hierarchy); COLLECTION = one file for all roots named after the collection, requires `collection`.
- Review fixes ruled at G3.5 (docs/REVIEW-B2-server.md RS-1..RS-12, contract-relevant ones): globs in `objects` are expanded against the `get_scene_info` names before the loop and default file stems are sanitised (`[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]` -> `_`; trailing underscores kept; `export` only when no alphanumeric remains, Grace's accepted deviation) (RS-4); the `collection` filter applies in BOTH the `objects` and the scene-roots branch (RS-5); `output_dir` resolves through `_default_output_path` (RS-11); the engine list is exactly H12's seven (no bare `GENERIC`, RS-2/RS-7); `driver_batch_max` can only LOWER the add-on's 200 (RS-6); a `driver_reply_max_chars` setting `<= 0` falls back to 24000 (RS-12); `find_mesh_issues(render=True)` reads the add-on's `image_error` (RS-3); `execute_blender_code` prints stdout beside the error block (RS-9).
- Reply text: one line per root `PASS|FAIL <name> -> <file> (<tris> tris)` or `FAIL <name>: <error>`, then `manifest: <output_dir>/batch_manifest.json` = `{engine, output_dir, written_at, rows: [{object, status: PASS|FAIL, filepath, triangles, error}]}` (server-written, H7).

#### 13.2.7 Settings and server

No new settings keys this phase. `output_dir` consumers gain `export_for_engine` and `batch_export` (file-leaving
tools, F13). New server banner `# --- Engine readiness ---`. Wrapper docstrings SHORT; the preset table is printed by
`get_export_preset`, not repeated. No `_drv_batch` / `_drv_op` helpers this phase (H4).

### 13.3 Facts (E1-E14 stand; new rows)

#### 13.3.1 E15-E18 (b2check2.py 018534df, both installs, `B2CHECK2` lines; feed conditional L4 Tier 2)

| # | Fact | 4.3.2 | 5.2.1 |
|---|---|---|---|
| E15 | `object.quadriflow_remesh` finishes synchronously headless; `mode` RATIO / EDGE / FACES; FACES target 500 on a 512-face sphere -> 469 quads; accepts open meshes and ngons; RATIO is not an input ratio; poll False in EDIT mode. `remesh_object(QUADRIFLOW)` exposes `target_faces` (FACES) only. | same | same |
| E16 | `object.voxel_remesh()` has no kwargs; reads `mesh.remesh_voxel_size / remesh_voxel_adaptivity / use_remesh_fix_poles / use_remesh_preserve_volume / use_remesh_preserve_attributes`; cube at 0.25 -> 486 quads. | `voxel_size 0.0` clamped to 1e-4 | stored 0.0 (the tool refuses `voxel_size <= 0` itself) |
| E17 | `mesh.separate(type=LOOSE)`: the source keeps its name and the first part; new objects `<name>.001/.002`; all stay selected, active = source, same collections. | same | same |
| E18 | `object.make_single_user(type=SELECTED_OBJECTS|ALL, object, obdata, material, animation, obdata_animation)`; one-object equivalent `obj.data = obj.data.copy()` keeps materials and custom props. | same | same |
| E20 (Linus RA landing, 19:49, both installs) | With an edit-mesh present (object in EDIT mode, incl. a co-edited object in a multi-object edit) `Mesh.uv_layers[0].data` has length 0 under RNA before AND after `update_from_editmode()`, while `bmesh.from_mesh` sees the UV layer; `update_from_editmode()` returns True and flushes geometry; the evaluated mesh of a co-edited object has 0 polygons in --background. Consequence: read handlers flush via `update_from_editmode` and read UVs from BMesh; `get_mesh_stats.triangles` is 0 for an object in EDIT mode (Blender behaviour). | same | same |
| E21 (Margaret B2-G5 live, 5.2.1 GUI, 2026-10-22 11:13) | (a) E20 holds in a GUI session: `get_mesh_stats` of the EDIT-mode object reports triangles 0 / triangles_simple 62. (b) In a GUI session Blender's own RNA update publishes property changes to msgbus subscribers, so a subscriber fires on `rna_set(publish=False)` as well as on `publish=True`; `publish=True` is observable only headless (R18), where it is the only path. Docs nuance (TOOLS.md rna_set line), not a defect. | GUI 5.2.1 (4.3.2 run pending) | same |
| E19 (R-B2-35 refined, Margaret 19:14, pure Blender, no add-on) | Blender's own FBX export with `use_triangles=True` drops ONE Suzanne face in any round trip: 968 -> 967 triangles, vertices 507 -> 507; the same after remove_doubles + dissolve_degenerate, and when pre-triangulated; `use_triangles=False` keeps 968; a 32x16 UV sphere round-trips 960 -> 960 exactly. The manifest keeps the evaluated count at export, so a user comparing manifest `triangles` with an engine import of a Suzanne-like mesh sees -1. Harness E2 fixture = the UV sphere (b2_steps db9241d2), no tolerance. Release notes carry this under "known Blender facts" (Grace, B2-D). | same | same |

```
export_object:  apply_modifiers axis_forward axis_up colors_type export_animation_mode export_apply

(get_mesh_stats, get_version: no param change)
```

#### 13.5.1 Risks and seams (each with the test that fails when it happens)

| Risk | Test that catches it |
|---|---|
| `validate_asset` reports PASS on an inside-out mesh (recalc dry-run compares against the live normals instead of a copy), or `fix=False` leaks a selection/mode change. | V2: flip all faces of a cube via `fix_normals(FLIP)` -> `inconsistent_normals` FAIL count 6; `fix=True` -> PASS; V1 runs with `readonly=True`. |
| Tangent export on an ngon mesh aborts the exporter (E5). | V2 + E2: cylinder with ngon caps -> `ngons` FAIL for UNITY; `export_for_engine(UNITY, triangulate=True)` writes the file and the source keeps its ngons (D6). |
| The temporary Triangulate modifier is left behind, or applied to the scene mesh. | E2: source polygon count unchanged after export; `obj.modifiers` unchanged; re-import triangles == manifest `triangles`. |
| `decimate_mesh(target_triangles)` applies per pass (destroys the mesh) instead of reading `face_count`. | O1: target on the 512-face sphere -> `passes <= 6`, within 5 %, source datablock unchanged when `new_object` given. |
| `generate_lods` renames the source and then fails, or overwrites an existing `<base>_LOD1`. | O2b: `ratios=2,abc` -> `{error}` with the source name and tri count intact; pre-existing `Suz_LOD1` -> `{error, existing}` and nothing renamed. |
| `create_collision` hull keeps interior geometry (E11 `geom_interior` not deleted) or leftover faces. | C1: torus CONVEX -> `volume_ratio` in [1.7, 2.3] (R-B2-34) and the hull is closed and manifold; mutant skip the delete -> FAIL. |
| `check_collision_fit` inside test has the wrong sign (reports 0 or 1 regardless). | C3: exact box proxy -> `covered_fraction > 0.95`; proxy scaled 0.5 -> `< 0.6` (Margaret's discriminating fixture). |
| `add_socket(TIMBERMESH)` accepts a parent chain without a `#` root (Timbermesh merges it away). | S1b: parent `Prop` -> `{error}`; parent `#Prop` -> `#Muzzle`. |
| `set_origin` / `set_pivot` / `apply_transforms` move the geometry in world space on a ROTATED object. | F6 / S3 / V10: world vertex coordinates identical before and after (Margaret headline 2). |
| `export_for_engine` exports despite FAIL, or writes a manifest on refusal. | E2b: unapplied-scale object -> `{error}`, no file, no manifest; `overrides` with force -> file + `validation.verdict == FAIL` in the manifest. |
| FBX round trip loses `UCX_` / `SOCKET_` names or custom props (E8 kwargs not passed). | E2: re-import in a fresh scene -> names and `lod_group` present. Fixture (R-B2-35 refined, E19): a 32x16 UV sphere named per the doc step (960 -> 960 exact); Suzanne loses one face in Blender's own FBX round trip with `use_triangles=True` regardless of doubles, no add-on code involved; the manifest keeps the evaluated count at export. |
| glTF preset drops tangents (E9) or vertex order (SPT lesson). | E3: GLB re-import -> `compare_meshes` `order_match` True on the unsplit fixture (H22), `max_delta < 1e-5`; the GLB JSON chunk contains TANGENT. |
| UNREAL scale warning inverted. | E2 / E6: `scale_length` 1.0 -> warning names `set_scene_units`; 0.01 -> no warning. |
| `batch_export` stops at the first failure with `continue_on_error=True`, or the manifest misses the FAIL row. | S5 (venv) + E5 (harness): 3 roots, 2nd invalid -> 2 files, rows PASS/FAIL/PASS. |
| Timbermesh presence keyed on `check()` or on version. | E4 on both installs branches on `enable()` success; M7 grep 0 (G2). |
| Legacy secret migration finds nothing after the StringProperties are unregistered (H12). | G7: both fixtures -> prefs hold the values, `scene.get(key)` and the SYSTEM group lack them. |
| `get_scene_info` growth breaks the settings fixture or a legacy step. | G3: 204 legacy PASS; `settings_baseline --check` match:true (S9). |
| Two version sources bumped, one forgotten. | `test_version_sync.py` with the 2.4.0 default, green at B2-G1 (R-B2-17 (4)). |
| A B2 handler leaves EDIT mode, a temp collection or a temp modifier behind. | `step()` invariant on every step; E4 asserts the Timbermesh temp collection gone. |
| Non-ASCII in a step detail kills the cp1252 console. | Margaret greps `b2_steps.py` for non-ASCII before T1 lands. |
| The 2.3.0 generic driver does not compose with the 2.4.0 tools (R-B2-40 / B2.16; the user's question). By construction it does: `drv_batch` dispatches ANY handler key, `transaction` rollback covers any mutation, the driver reads everything the B2 tools create; `batch_export` is a server loop, so it is a tool call, never a batch item. | B2-G8 (Margaret, review landing): a `batch` with `$id` substitution over `validate_asset` -> `decimate_mesh` -> `create_collision`; `batch(undo=AUTO)` rolling back a B2 mutation; `transaction` BEGIN -> `set_pivot` + `add_socket` -> ROLLBACK restores both; `rna_get` on a `UCX_` proxy by `#uid`. Release notes (Grace, B2-D): "every 2.4.0 tool is batchable and transactional through the driver". |

MEASURED 2026-10-21 (Linus, both installs): (a) after unregistering a Scene StringProperty, a 4.3.2-saved value
comes back via `scene.get(key)` and `getattr` raises; a 5.2.1-saved value sits in the SYSTEM idprop group,
`scene.get()` is None, `scene.bl_system_properties_get(do_create=False)` reaches it (4.3.2 lacks the method) -> H12.
(b) `addon_utils.check(timbermesh_blender_plugin)` is (False, False) on both installs -> H17. (c) depsgraph
update uid = `update.id.original.session_uid` (`update.id` is the evaluated copy, uid 0); the master Scene
Collection update has `original is None` (guard). (d) `render_write` for a STILL writes
`bpy.path.ensure_ext(render.filepath, ext)`; `frame_path()` only for animations. (e) msgbus delivers nothing
headless on set or `publish_rna` (R18 stands). (f) All 12 handler names exist on both installs and FIRE headless
with `@persistent`: depsgraph_update_post only when something changed (0 on a no-op `view_layer.update()`),
frame_change_post 1 per `frame_set`, render_pre/post/write/complete 1 each per still and per 1-frame animation
(render_cancel 0), save_pre/save_post 1 each, load_post 1, undo_post/redo_post 1 each after `ed.undo`/`ed.redo`
(undo really reverts); handler args (Scene, Depsgraph) for depsgraph/frame, (Scene, None) render/undo, (str,
None) save/load; persistent handlers survive `open_mainfile`; `deque(maxlen)` overflow drops the OLDEST and a
rebuild with another maxlen keeps order. Evidence: `evcheck.py` 43f98c7b, lines under `### evcheck.py` in
`out_<ver>.jsonl`, EXIT 0 both, handler errors 0. The table below is therefore ANSWERED; it stays as the index:

| # | Question | Test that settles it |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Which of `depsgraph_update_post, frame_change_post, render_pre, render_post, render_write, render_complete, render_cancel, save_pre, save_post, load_post, undo_post, redo_post` exist on each install and FIRE headless: `ed.undo`/`ed.redo` after an `undo_push`; `wm.save_as_mainfile(temp)`; `wm.open_mainfile(that file)`; `render.render(write_still=True)` at 8x8 WORKBENCH; `frame_set`; `view_layer.update()`. | Append a counter lambda to each, trigger, print counts. |
| 2 | `depsgraph.updates[i].id.session_uid` readable for every update kind inside the handler (object move, rename, collection link)? | Read uids in the handler, count exceptions. |
| 3 | `scene.render.frame_path()` inside `render_write` equals the file written? | Compare with the file on disk. |
| 4 | msgbus delivers nothing headless even when `subscribe_rna` + `publish_rna` run in the same timer tick (re-confirm R18 against 2.3.0's `rna_set(publish=True)`). | One line. |
| 5 | A handler appended twice (reload idiom `if fn not in handlers`) fires once? | Register twice, `frame_set`, count. |
| 6 | `collections.deque(maxlen)` rebuilt with a larger maxlen keeps order (stdlib sanity). | One assert. |

| L-Q1 (B2.9, from Linus L1 deviation a) | RULED R-B2-29 (18:45): `validate_asset.non_manifold` EXCLUDES boundary edges; it counts edges with more than 2 faces, wire edges and non-contiguous (opposed-winding) edges; boundary edges go into the check's `detail` as `boundary: <n>` and stay PASS. `get_mesh_stats` keeps BOTH `non_manifold_edges` (Blender's `is_manifold == False`, boundary included) and `boundary_edges`; `find_mesh_issues(NON_MANIFOLD)` keeps every `select_non_manifold` flag (BOUNDARY is its own issue). Linus L1.1 = the filter, re-hash, both installs. | - | Decisive fact (Lead): foliage cards, decals and trim pieces (request doc categories 8 and 13) are open meshes by design. Test (Margaret V-step): a plane -> `non_manifold` PASS with `boundary: 4` in detail; a wire edge -> WARN. |

F-B2-1 (finding, Linus L1 deviation i): the add-on returns `offset_local` where the contract (13.2.1) and Grace's L1.2 wrapper read `offset_applied`; the server prints nothing today. Fix at the root = rename the add-on key to `offset_applied` (contract wins; one line), oracle F6 keys on it.

| L-Q2 (B2.10, from Grace L3.1) | RULED R-B2-30 = option A (18:49): `get_scene_info` entries gain `collections: [names]` (`users_collection`) in Linus L1.1; `batch_export(collection=...)` filters on it; `drv_find` leaves the server loop entirely. Tests: F4 asserts `collections == ["Collection"]` on a default-scene cube; S5 asserts ONE send before the loop. | - | Decisive fact: R-B2-28's reason (one additive key beats a second round trip). |

F-B2-3 (finding, Margaret's B2-G8 part (c), 19:40; driver, 2.3.0 code in the same tree, owner Linus; CLOSED 19:51 at addon ee9b9dc0, B2-G8 (c) green on both installs): `transaction` ROLLBACK does NOT revert Python-only mutations (a `drv_set` of location, a `set_origin`) when the undo stack was exhausted by a previous rollback: measured at addon 94cb47dc on 4.3.2, the next transaction's ROLLBACK reports `undo_calls: 1` and leaves location at 7,7,7; with an explicit undo push before the ROLLBACK (`undo_calls: 2`) it reverts; a set followed by an operator (the DRV-7 shape) reverts. Decisive fact: `undo_push` only records state at push time, so Python-only edits made after the BEGIN push are not on the stack until something pushes again. RULED R-B2-42 (19:40, B2.19): `bpy.ops.ed.undo_push(message="mcp:<name>:rollback")` before `_drv_unwind` in `drv_transaction` ROLLBACK, and in the `batch(undo=AUTO)` path if it shares the unwind; Linus folds it into the RA landing; harness B2-G8 (c) is the regression guard. Section 12's R22 sequence therefore reads: BEGIN = `undo_push(pre)` -> marker -> `undo_push(name)`; ROLLBACK = `undo_push(name:rollback)` -> `ed.undo` until the marker is gone. As built (B2.20): the closing push lives INSIDE `_drv_unwind(marker, seq, label)` (`mcp:<name>:rollback` / `mcp:batch:rollback`), so transaction ROLLBACK and `batch(undo=AUTO)` both get it; `undo_calls` for a plain transaction rollback is now 2 (DRV-7 asserts >= 1). Release notes (Grace, B2-D): "2.3.0 driver fix: transaction ROLLBACK (and batch undo=AUTO) now reverts Python-only mutations made after BEGIN; found by the 2.4.0 interop step".

F-B2-4 (finding, Margaret B2-G5 live 5.2.1, 2026-10-22 11:13; add-on, 2.3.0 driver code in the same uncommitted tree, owner Linus, severity low; RULED R-B2-43 08:14 = option A below plus a live probe, B2.21): `execute_blender_code(result_expr=<dict expression>)` returns only the dict KEYS: `"{'a': 1, 'b': [1, 2]}"` -> `["a", "b"]`; `"[{'a': 1}, dict(b=2)]"` -> `[["a"], ["b"]]`. Root cause VERIFIED by the Planner at addon ee9b9dc0: `_encode_value` (addon.py:9116) has branches for None / ID / Matrix / bpy_prop_collection / bpy_struct / set / scalars and then a generic `__len__` iteration, no `dict` branch, so a dict iterates its keys. `result_expr` and `_encode_value` are both absent from the committed 2.2.0 (HEAD), so this is a 2.3.0 bug surfaced by the 2.4.0 live pass; the only harness step on `result_expr` (DRV-12b) covers a scalar and an ID, never a dict. The same encoder serves `rna_get` / `scene_snapshot` / batch replies, so any dict-valued Python object (not RNA; RNA has no dict properties) is affected the same way. Fix (one branch, before the `__len__` branch): `if isinstance(value, dict): return {str(k): self._encode_value(v, None, depth, None, angles) for k, v in value.items()}`. Regression guard: one step in drv_steps.py (owner Margaret) asserting `result_expr="{'a': 1, 'b': [1, 2]}"` round-trips as `{"a": 1, "b": [1, 2]}` and the nested-list form as `[{"a": 1}, {"b": 2}]`, red at ee9b9dc0, green after the fix. Options for the Lead: (A) REOPEN the pair after the 4.3.2 live run finishes on ee9b9dc0 (keeps every G5 evidence line on one pair): Linus adds the branch (addon.py only), Margaret adds the step (drv_steps.py only), harness both installs headless (253/253 expected), venv 130 unchanged (server.py untouched, tools/list identical, identity test stays green), settings --check unchanged, then RE-FREEZE with the two new hashes and a 13.11 row; no live re-run needed since the encoder has no GUI dependence. Cost about two both-install cycles. (B) DEFER to 13.10 as a 2.4.x item, with a known-limit line in the release notes and TOOLS.md ("result_expr dicts return their keys; return a list or a scalar"). RECOMMENDATION: A. Decisive fact: the user's standing directive for this stage is "do a code review and fix each issue", and the fix is a three-line add-on branch with a one-step guard; B ships a documented wrong answer for a tool agents reach for first. The test that distinguishes A from B is the DRV-12c step above: it is red at ee9b9dc0 and must be green at the re-frozen add-on. R-B2-43 (Lead, 08:14, B2.21): FIXED this stage (user directive: fix every issue). Fix = one `isinstance(value, dict)` branch in `_encode_value` returning `{str(k): encode(v)}` BEFORE the sequence branch, dicts only (an `IDPropertyGroup` is not a dict; driver replies unchanged). Guard = headless DRV-12c asserting a dict `result_expr` round-trips, the nested list of dicts too. SEQUENCE (the pair is REOPENED for addon.py and drv_steps.py only, nothing else): Linus prepares the fix + a mutant on a SCRATCH copy and lands into addon.py ONLY after Margaret posts that the 4.3.2 live run is done (her GUI loaded the frozen file at start; the tree stays untouched until then); then Margaret adds DRV-12c; both-install harness at the new add-on hash (expect 253/253), venv 130 unchanged, settings --check unchanged, tools/list unchanged (server.py untouched); RE-FREEZE = one new add-on hash + one new drv_steps hash, 13.11 row; a 2-minute live probe of `execute_blender_code(result_expr=<dict>)` on one GUI instance at the new hash closes G5 (every live item verified at the shipped hash). Release notes (Grace at her resume, with the E21 rna_set nuance): "2.3.0 driver fix: execute_blender_code result_expr now returns dicts as JSON objects (keys only before); found by the 2.4.0 live pass".

F-B2-6 (finding, Margaret B2-G5 live, 4.3.2 GUI only, 2026-10-22 11:20; add-on, owner Linus, severity MEDIUM; RULED R-B2-44 08:20 = option A, B2.22; F-B2-5 is the server finding below, filed on card b2-review-server): the capture tools do not restore the user's viewport on 4.3.2. Root cause VERIFIED by the Planner at ee9b9dc0: `capture_viewport_angle` (addon.py:1429-1441) saves and restores only `r3d.view_matrix`, `view_perspective` and `smooth_view`; on 4.3.2 assigning `view_matrix` does not write back `view_location` / `view_rotation` / `view_distance`, so the next redraw recomputes the matrix from the capture framing. Every capture caller routes through it (contact sheet :1530, `find_mesh_issues(render=True)` :2859, `find_unweighted_vertices` render :8158 / :8180), so one fix. The committed 2.2.0 (HEAD) restore block has the same three fields and no `view_location`, so this is 2.2.0 behaviour already live, surfaced by the 2.4.0 live pass. Measured (plan_r, 4.3.2 GUI): view_all -> view_location (3.5, -3.5, 4.0) dist 32.928; after `find_mesh_issues(render=True)` -> (3.5, -3.5, 33.462) dist 3.466, viewport empty; after `capture_contact_sheet` -> (0, 5, -1.486) dist 3.466; after `capture_viewport_angle(iso_front_right)` -> (3.5, -3.5, 33.462) dist 3.466. On 5.2.1 the same calls leave the view intact. Fix shape VERIFIED LIVE by QA (plan_t): save `(view_location.copy(), view_rotation.copy(), view_distance, view_perspective)` before and assign them back in the `finally` at :1440 (keeping the matrix restore as today) restores (3.5, -3.5, 4.0) / 32.928 on 4.3.2 and the screenshot shows the scene again; no version compare needed (M7 stays 0). Guard: headless cannot see it (no window); the live list gets a 4.3.2 item: `view_location` / `view_rotation` / `view_distance` equal before and after each of the three capture callers. Options: (A) fold into the already-open addon.py reopen (R-B2-43): the same Linus landing carries F-B2-4 + F-B2-6, red proof = Margaret's plan_r measurement at ee9b9dc0, green = the same probe at the new hash on the 4.3.2 GUI (adds a few minutes to the closing live probe); harness unchanged (252 + DRV-12c). (B) defer to 13.10 as 2.2.0 behaviour, fixed in a 2.4.x. RECOMMENDATION: A. Decisive fact: it is user-visible (an empty viewport after any render-assisted check), the fix is four saved fields in a `finally` that is being landed anyway, and QA has already proven the fix shape live, so A costs one probe and B costs a known-limit line plus a second reopen later.

F-B2-7 (finding, Margaret B2-G5 live, 4.3.2 with the Timbermesh plug-in present, 2026-10-22 11:20; add-on, 2.4.0 code, owner Linus, severity LOW; RULED R-B2-44 08:20 = option A with the b2_steps.py E4 assert, B2.22): `export_for_engine(TIMBERMESH)` leaves `scene.frame_current` at 0 (plan_s: frame 1 -> UNREAL export -> still 1 -> TIMBERMESH export -> 0). Root cause VERIFIED by the Planner at ee9b9dc0: `_export_timbermesh` (addon.py:4452) enables the plug-in and calls its exporter with no frame save; the plug-in's single-animation export rewinds the frame. `_settings_scope` (addon.py:4705) already snapshots `scene.frame_current` for renders. Fix: save `scene.frame_current` before the plug-in call in `_export_timbermesh` and restore it in a `finally` (two lines; the full `_settings_scope` would also snapshot RENDER / OUTPUT / CYCLES / EEVEE around an export, which is more than the defect needs). Guard: headless E4 can assert `scene.frame_current` unchanged after the TIMBERMESH export where `enable()` succeeds (4.3.2); that is a b2_steps.py edit, which the R-B2-43 reopen scope (addon.py + drv_steps.py) does not cover. Options: (A) fold into the same Linus landing and WIDEN the reopen scope to b2_steps.py for the one E4 assert (Margaret owns both step files; b2_steps gets a new hash, 253/253 stays the harness expectation since E4 is an extended step, not a new one). (B) land the add-on fix in the same landing and keep the guard on the live list only (no b2_steps edit, scope stays as ruled). (C) defer to 13.10. RECOMMENDATION: A. Decisive fact: a headless assert on E4 is the only guard that runs on every future landing; the live list runs once per stage, and the fix itself is two lines in a function already being reopened.

Sequencing note for the Lead (Planner): with A for both, the R-B2-43 sequence changes in three places only: (1) Linus's single addon.py landing carries F-B2-4 + F-B2-6 + F-B2-7 (three independent hunks, one hash); (2) Margaret's step edits = DRV-12c (drv_steps.py) + the E4 frame assert (b2_steps.py), two new hashes; (3) the closing live probe on one 4.3.2 GUI instance covers three items: `execute_blender_code(dict)`, viewport fields equal before/after a capture, `frame_current` unchanged after a TIMBERMESH export. Re-freeze = one add-on hash + two step-file hashes; the server, venv count and tools/list stay untouched. SUPERSEDED in part by R-B2-45 below: the reopen also covers server.py (F-B2-5) and test_b2_server.py, so the re-freeze = addon + server + drv_steps + b2_steps + test_b2_server hashes, venv 131 (130 + the FakeConn case), tools/list still byte-identical to the 2.4 json (no schema change).

F-B2-6: `capture_viewport_angle` saves and restores `view_location.copy()`, `view_rotation.copy()`, `view_distance`, `view_perspective` AND the matrix in its `finally` (:1440), no version compare; no headless guard exists (no window), so the live list gains: `view_location` / `view_distance` unchanged after `find_mesh_issues(render=True)`, `capture_contact_sheet` and `capture_viewport_angle` on 4.3.2. F-B2-7: the Timbermesh exporter call saves and restores `scene.frame_current` (the `_settings_scope` shape; Linus's prepared form = record `frame_current` before the plug-in call and `frame_set()` it back in the `finally` when it moved, the "or" form of the ruling, accepted); headless guard = E4 asserts `scene.frame_current` unchanged after the TIMBERMESH export (Margaret, b2_steps.py, with DRV-12c). Linus's scratch 2a97736c (on top of 5cac375d) carries all three: F-B2-4 smoke 4/4, F-B2-7 smoke on 4.3.2 frame 7 -> 0 RED at ee9b9dc0 / 7 -> 7 GREEN at 2a97736c; mutants mut_f7 773995d0 (frame restore removed, smoke RED) and mut_f6 11b306b1 (view-field restore removed; live re-probe mutant only, no headless discriminator).

F-B2-5 (finding, Margaret B2-G5 live 5.2.1, 2026-10-22 11:20, card b2-review-server; server, owner Grace, severity LOW but a false claim; RULED R-B2-45 08:21, B2.22): `close_blender(force=True)` against a Blender the server did NOT start replied "Blender closed (process terminated)." while nothing was terminated: with `force=True` the graceful `quit_blender` path was skipped, `_blender_process` was None so nothing was killed, and the reply text picked "process terminated" because of an `elif pid or force`. Measured on the 5.2.1 QA instance at server a2cdd77f: `force=True` -> "process terminated" and 8 s later pid 16436 was still alive and listening on 9877; `force=False` -> "Blender closed (gracefully)." and the process exited. R-B2-45: with no managed process, `force=True` sends `quit_blender` over the socket like `force=False` and the reply reads "Blender asked to quit (not a process this server started; force applies only to managed processes)."; the "terminated" text only after a real kill. Venv guard (Margaret, test_b2_server.py): FakeConn case with `_blender_process` None + `force=True` asserts a `quit_blender` send and no "terminated" text; venv 130 -> 131. Grace RESUMED 08:21 for the hunk, the TOOLS.md `rna_set` sentence (E21 b) and the release-notes lines for F-B2-4/5/6/7. Planner observation 08:2x: server.py on disk already reads 78df0099 with the R-B2-45 comment, the `(not force or not managed)` guard and the "asked to quit" text (one hunk; QA verification and the identity test pending), so the frozen a2cdd77f state is gone from the tree as the reopen allows.

F-B2-2 (finding, Grace L3 deviation 1; CLOSED by L3.1 server d80f593f): `batch_export` still issues the `drv_find` fallback as a second send because the add-on hash she read (1c43d6b1) had no `parent` key; R-B2-28 (B2.7) ruled ONE `get_scene_info` send and Linus's landed ee6b927f carries `parent`. Fix at the root = drop the `drv_find` send, resolve roots from the `get_scene_info` entries (type MESH, parent null, hidden when skip_hidden, collection membership), re-hash; Margaret S5 asserts exactly one send before the loop.

Grace L3 deviations folded as text (B2.10): (2) `one_file_per`: ROOT behaves as OBJECT (each root with its hierarchy); COLLECTION = ONE file for all roots named after the collection and requires `collection` (refused before connect otherwise). (3) `location` / `rotation` strings of `add_socket`, `set_pivot`, `snap_to_grid` are validated server-side (`_parse_vec3`) and forwarded as the typed "x,y,z" string; `set_origin` forwards a `[x,y,z]` list; the add-on accepts BOTH forms for every vector param (one `_vec3_arg` helper). (4) `export_for_engine` has `engine` as its first (REQUIRED) parameter, so the schema's `required == ["engine"]`; `objects` / `collection` follow.

### 13.12 After-action reports (one paragraph + one rule each; collected as they land)

**Planner (Barbara)**, written at the B2.12 resume, final at B2-G4: The contract went from a 25-tool option-A draft to
the ruled 22-tool stage in three rewrites in the first hour, and then held: every later amendment (B2.5-B2.12) was one
or two clauses that a coder's measured fact or a reviewer's finding forced, never a redesign. What paid: carrying the
previous phase's helpers and idioms by file:line instead of inventing seams; the oracle written before the handlers
existed, so QA's steps and the coders' self-checks keyed on one text; folding every declared deviation into the
contract the minute it was posted, so the server and add-on never disagreed on a key name for more than one landing
(the two times they did, F-B2-1 and F-B2-2, the finding was a one-line rename or a dropped send). What cost: two
contract rows I wrote from a stale read (get_scene_info had no parent/collections keys; the E11 hull ratio came from a
check script that had summed leftover faces), each of which became a ruling only after a coder measured it; a Bash
heredoc that put a backspace into a path. RULE: before a row names a reply key, grep the handler on disk for it; before
a row names a number, re-run the check script that produced it on the fixture the test will use.

**Add-on (Linus)** (verbatim, forwarded by the Lead 19:51): I landed four slices on one file (L1 f2c680bb -> ee6b927f -> 393390af, L2 a611e0e3, L3 e9c78fe0, review ee9b9dc0), 177 -> 192 handlers, every landing green on both installs with settings byte-identical, and every landing carried mutants on scratch copies so each claim had a red to show. What worked: recon before code (the apichecks scripts settled the 5.x ID-property groups, the FBX triangle loss and the evaluated-count facts before any handler was written), building on the existing idioms (_selection_scope, _mode_restore, _bmesh_read, _names_or_collection) instead of new mechanisms, and a private smoke script run before every harness run, which found two real defects the harness could not see (RA-2 crash on a co-edited mesh, compare_meshes reading stale edit data) and two test-side defects. What cost time: three unread DMs early on (fixed by polling the inbox before every step), two hypotheses posted before measuring (the FBX weld cause, the hull volume window) that a probe overturned, and step files moving under a landing run three times. Facts worth keeping: a mesh in EDIT mode exposes zero-length loop layers under RNA while a bmesh copy sees them; update_from_editmode() flushes geometry without a mode change; the evaluated mesh of a co-edited object has 0 polygons headless; Blender's undo only captures state at a push so a rollback needs a closing push; "[" in an object name is a glob class to fnmatch. ONE RULE: before a landing run, write a ten-line smoke that exercises each fix with the user's state deliberately wrong (another object in EDIT mode, a shared mesh, a name with glob characters, a Python-only mutation before ROLLBACK), run it on both installs, and break the fix on a scratch copy to see that smoke go red; the shared harness proves the contract, only the hostile-state smoke proves the fix.

**Server + docs (Grace)** (verbatim, forwarded by the Lead 19:51): The server slice went in three thin landings (6 + 8 + 8 wrappers, 182 -> 204) against a contract I had read end to end before GO; each landing carried a FakeConn self-check that asserted the exact wire tuple and the zero-send refusal paths before I posted a hash, and one mutant per landing had to turn that self-check red before I called it evidence; that is why QA's 35 server cases were green on first contact every time except where the contract itself had moved. The pre-GO cross-read paid for itself twice (test-plan vs s13 conflict on manifest + preset ownership; the apply_modifier required-list hazard), and the recon fact that get_scene_info entries had no parent key became R-B2-28 instead of a bug. What cost time: the contract moved nine times after GO, three of those cost a landing plus a re-hash; one fallback built against a superseded ruling was withdrawn in the same minute only because the Lead's STOP was urgent. The review pass found 15 real server items, two of which (GENERIC engine KeyError, the 10-object scene cap under batch_export) my self-checks could not see because they asserted the wire, never the add-on's reply cap or the engine map's keys. What I would change: freeze the add-on reply-key list and the entry keys of get_scene_info in the contract BEFORE the server slice starts; every server red this stage was a key the add-on did or did not send, never logic. RULE: before the first wrapper, write the FakeConn fixture from the contract's reply rows AND its stated caps/limits (object-list caps, enum tables, key presence), run it red against the committed server, and keep it in the repo tests, not a scratchpad; a wire-tuple check proves you sent the right thing, only a reply-shape-plus-limits check proves you can read what comes back.

**QA (Margaret)** (verbatim, forwarded by the Lead 2026-10-22 08:01): What worked: asserting the contract instead of the code (G8 found a real 2.3.0 driver bug the moment the user asked whether the driver composes with the new tools), red-before-green on every step with run-time hashes in the log head, and mutant copies cut by the owner but RUN by QA, which turned two smoke-only mutants into harness-discriminated ones in one edit. What cost time: my own test-side mistakes (a dict compared to a list, a heredoc that swallowed a line continuation) each cost a full both-install cycle, and the exec-loaded step files meant every edit had to be sequenced around someone else's run by DM. What I would change: cut a tiny read-shape probe (one call, print the reply keys) before writing any new assert against a reply I have not printed in this session, and keep the step files owner-locked with an explicit 'yours now' handshake from the first run, not the fourth. ONE RULE for the next team's QA: never write an assert against a reply shape you have not printed in this session at the current hash; a 20-second probe is cheaper than a 6-minute both-install red. QA sign-off line (13.11): every 13.2.x criterion verified with evidence on disk; findings open = none; not verifiable headless = the G5 live list. AAR ADDENDUM after the G5 live pass (forwarded by the Lead 2026-10-23): on a live probe, read the UI state fields (view_location / view_rotation / view_distance, mode, active, frame_current) around every capture or export call; the screenshot can lie while the numbers cannot (F-B2-6 showed as blank 4.3.2 screenshots that the view-field readback explained in one bisect, and F-B2-7 was invisible in any image).
